SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                    64
   TIP - The 260 px ceiling is what keeps this scanner quiet on real sites. Raising it pulls hero logos and article images into stage one
   and multiplies false candidates; if you extend ISSUER_TABLE, prefer tightening the vocabulary words over loosening the size
   bound.



Part 4 - Form Guardian

4.1 Design analysis
Form Guardian is the first module allowed to say INTERCEPTED, and it earns it with hard evidence: a form on the page you
are looking at is about to send your password or card number to a *different origin* than the one in your address bar. That
single pattern - visible origin A, credential destination B - is the mechanics of most phishing pages ever built, and it is
checkable deterministically at submit time, which is why this module intercepts instead of annotating. Everything else about
the design follows from the discipline that interception must never be wrong AND never be permanent: the layer always
offers Stop and Continue, the form's contents are untouched, and Continue re-fires the real submit event with the module
stepping aside.

Field detection leans on the platform before heuristics: input[type=password] is definitive for credentials; card fields are
identified by autocomplete hints (cc-*) or the well-known name/id vocabulary plus numeric input modes. Action resolution
leans on the platform too - the form.action IDL property returns an absolutely-resolved URL even when the attribute is
empty or relative (the trap that catches naive getAttribute('action') implementations, which read "" and mis-blame the
page's own origin). Approval memory is salted and one-way: the service worker stores SHA-256(salt + destination origin),
never the origin text, so the "sites I already trust with this" list is useless if exfiltrated.

 Submit pattern                                                  Behavior                         Rationale

 Password field, action origin == page origin                    silent                           The normal case: 99.9% of logins

 Password field, action origin != page origin, not approved      full-page INTERCEPTED            The phishing mechanic, caught exactly
                                                                 card

 Card hints, cross-origin action                                 full-page INTERCEPTED            Card numbers deserve the same bar
                                                                 card

 Cross-origin POST to SSO allowlist                              silent                           Legitimate handoff flows
 (login.microsoftonline.com, ...)

 Previously approved destination (salted hash match)             silent                           User already decided; no nagging

 Same-origin action but page is itself suspicious                nothing here                     Link/Site modules' job, not Form
                                                                                                  Guardian's


Chrome APIs used: none in the content world beyond the bridge; the approval-hash route runs in the service worker using
crypto.subtle and chrome.storage.local. Popup row: "Form Guardian - watching 2 forms" / "1 password form on this
page". Options entries: "remember approved destinations" toggle (on by default), an "approved destinations" count with a
Clear button (Part 11). Toggle-off cleanup: the intercept layer is removed if present, submit listeners detach, and any form
whose native submission was prevented gets its submit event re-dispatched with the bypass flag set - the page is left exactly as
found, mid-submit and unharmed.


4.2 Files




SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                                    65
  modules/form-guardian/form-guardian.js
/* modules/form-guardian/form-guardian.js - credential exfiltration
 * stop. The only module allowed to escalate to INTERCEPTED alongside
 * Download Guard. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (!globalThis.SL || !SL.__bridge) return;

  /* Legitimate cross-origin SSO handoffs - never warn on these. */
  const SSO_ACTION_ALLOWLIST = [
     'login.microsoftonline.com', 'login.live.com',
     'accounts.google.com', 'appleid.apple.com',
     'auth0.com', 'okta.com', 'secure.login.gov'
  ];

  const CARD_AUTOCOMPLETE = /^cc-(name|number|csc|exp|type|cvc)/i;
  const CARD_NAME_RE = /(card|cc|cvc|cvv|csc|expir|pan)[_\-]?/i;
  let cfg = { rememberApproved: true };
  let bypass = false;            // set while re-dispatching after Continue
  let layer = null;              // the intercept DOM
  let heldForm = null;           // form awaiting the user's decision
  let watching = 0;

  function originOf(url) {
    try { return new URL(url, location.href).origin; } catch (e) {
      return location.origin;
    }
  }

  function isSensitive(form) {
    if (form.querySelector('input[type=password]')) return 'password';
    for (const el of form.querySelectorAll('input, select')) {
      const ac = el.getAttribute('autocomplete') || '';
      if (CARD_AUTOCOMPLETE.test(ac)) return 'card';
      const name = (el.name || '') + ' ' + (el.id || '');
      const numeric = (el.getAttribute('inputmode') === 'numeric' ||
        /^\d[\d\s\-]{11,23}$/.test(el.value || ''));
      if (CARD_NAME_RE.test(name) && numeric) return 'card';
    }
    return null;
  }

  function allowlisted(actionOrigin) {
    const host = new URL(actionOrigin).hostname;
    return SSO_ACTION_ALLOWLIST.some((h) =>
      host === h || host.endsWith('.' + h));
  }

  /* The IDL property form.action is ALWAYS an absolute URL - this is
    * the correct way to resolve empty/relative action attributes. */
  function actionUrl(form) {
     try { return new URL(form.action || location.href, location.href); }
     catch (e) { return new URL(location.href); }
  }

  function onSubmit(e) {
    if (bypass) return;               // our own re-dispatch
    const form = e.target;
    if (!form || form.tagName !== 'FORM') return;
    const kind = isSensitive(form);
    if (!kind) return;
    const action = actionUrl(form);
    const destOrigin = action.origin;
    if (destOrigin === location.origin) return;        // normal case
    if (allowlisted(destOrigin)) return;                // SSO handoff

     // Hard evidence + credentials in flight -> INTERCEPTED.
     e.preventDefault();
     e.stopImmediatePropagation();
     heldForm = form;
     SL.verdict(action.href, { credentials: true, escalate: true,
                               displayText: kind })
       .then((v) => showLayer(form, action, kind, v));



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                      66
  }

  function showLayer(form, action, kind, verdict) {
    removeLayer();
    layer = document.createElement('div');
    layer.className = 'sl-intercept sl-root';

      const card = document.createElement('div');
      card.className = 'sl-intercept__card';
      const title = document.createElement('div');
      title.className = 'sl-intercept__title';
      title.textContent = 'Your ' +
        (kind === 'password' ? 'password' : 'card number') +
        ' is about to leave this site';
      const body = document.createElement('div');
      body.className = 'sl-intercept__body';
      const destHost = action.hostname;
      body.textContent = 'You are on ' + location.hostname +
        ', but this form sends what you type to ' + destHost +
        '. If that surprises you, Stop and look again.';

      const actions = document.createElement('div');
      actions.className = 'sl-intercept__actions';

      const stop = document.createElement('button');
      stop.className = 'sl-btn sl-btn--danger';
      stop.type = 'button';
      stop.textContent = 'Stop';
      stop.addEventListener('click', () => {
        removeLayer();
        heldForm = null;
        SL.status('formGuardian', 'blocked a ' + kind + ' to ' + destHost);
      });

      const go = document.createElement('button');
      go.className = 'sl-btn';
      go.type = 'button';
      go.textContent = 'Continue';
      go.addEventListener('click', () => {
        proceed(cfg.rememberApproved !== false);
      });

      actions.appendChild(stop);
      actions.appendChild(go);
      card.appendChild(title);
      card.appendChild(body);
      if (verdict.reasons && verdict.reasons.length) {
        const why = document.createElement('div');
        why.className = 'sl-card__reason';
        why.textContent = verdict.reasons[0];
        card.appendChild(why);
      }
      card.appendChild(actions);
      layer.appendChild(card);
      (document.body || document.documentElement).appendChild(layer);
      stop.focus();
  }

  function proceed(remember) {
    const form = heldForm;
    removeLayer();
    heldForm = null;
    if (!form) return;
    if (remember) {
      const action = actionUrl(form);
      SL.send('formGuardian', 'rememberOrigin', { origin: action.origin });
    }
    // Re-fire the submit event with the module standing aside:
    // requestSubmit() runs the page's own handlers and validation,
    // then performs the native submission - the faithful path.
    bypass = true;
    try {
      if (typeof form.requestSubmit === 'function') form.requestSubmit();
      else form.submit();               // legacy fallback



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                      67
      } finally {
        bypass = false;
      }
  }

  function removeLayer() {
    if (layer) { layer.remove(); layer = null; }
  }

  function countForms() {
    const forms = document.querySelectorAll('form');
    let sensitive = 0;
    for (const f of forms) if (isSensitive(f)) sensitive++;
    watching = forms.length;
    SL.status('formGuardian', sensitive
      ? sensitive + ' sensitive form' + (sensitive > 1 ? 's' : '') +
        ' watched on this page'
      : 'watching ' + watching + ' forms');
  }

  let formObserver = null;

  function attach() {
    document.addEventListener('submit', onSubmit, true);
    countForms();
    formObserver = new MutationObserver(() => countForms());
    formObserver.observe(document.documentElement || document.body, {
      childList: true, subtree: true
    });
  }

  function detach() {
    document.removeEventListener('submit', onSubmit, true);
    if (formObserver) { formObserver.disconnect(); formObserver = null; }
    removeLayer();
    heldForm = null;
    SL.status('formGuardian', 'off');
  }

  SL.boot('formGuardian', {
    onEnabled(config) { cfg = Object.assign(cfg, config); attach(); },
    onConfig(next) { cfg = Object.assign(cfg, next || {}); },
    onDisabled() { detach(); }
  });
  SL.registerCleanup('formGuardian', detach);
})();

  modules/form-guardian/form-guardian.css
/* modules/form-guardian/form-guardian.css
 * The intercept layer is built entirely from components.css classes
 * (.sl-intercept, .sl-intercept__card, __title, __body, __actions). */



4.3 Background route + registry entry
Two small edits land with this part. First, the router route that stores salted approval hashes - paste this block anywhere after
the register('engine', ...) block in background/module-router.js. Second, the registry entry.

  background/module-router.js - add after the engine routes
  /* Approved credential destinations, stored as salted hashes. */
  register('formGuardian', {
    async rememberOrigin(payload) {
      const salt = await SL.Storage.ensureSalt();
      const data = new TextEncoder().encode(salt + payload.origin);
      const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', data);
      const hash = Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest), (b) =>
        b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
      const list = await SL.Storage.localGet('sl.approved', []);
      if (!list.includes(hash)) {
        list.push(hash);
        await SL.Storage.localSet('sl.approved', list.slice(-300));



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                       68
        }
        return { stored: true };
    },
    async originApproved(payload) {
       const salt = await SL.Storage.ensureSalt();
       const data = new TextEncoder().encode(salt + payload.origin);
       const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', data);
       const hash = Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest), (b) =>
         b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
       const list = await SL.Storage.localGet('sl.approved', []);
       return { approved: list.includes(hash) };
    },
    async clearApproved() {
       await SL.Storage.localSet('sl.approved', []);
       return { cleared: true };
    }
  });

     { id: 'formGuardian', name: 'Form Guardian', pillar: 'clickSafe',
       blurb: 'Stops passwords leaving for the wrong domain.',
       defaultEnabled: true,

         id: 'sl-cs-form-guardian', matches: ['http://*/*', 'https://*/*'],
         runAt: 'document_idle', world: 'ISOLATED', allFrames: false,
         js: ['shared/messaging.js', 'shared/cs-bridge.js',
              'ui/toast.js', 'ui/verdict-card.js',
              'modules/form-guardian/form-guardian.js'],

               'modules/form-guardian/form-guardian.css']
       }] },

 • Reload the extension; confirm sl-cs-form-guardian in the SW console registration log.
 • Open any site with a real login form (your webmail, a social network) and submit it: nothing should appear - same-origin
   submits are the silent majority.
 • Inject a hostile form from DevTools and submit it: var f=document.createElement('form');f.method='POST';f.
    action='https://httpbin.org/post';f.innerHTML='<input name=p type=password><button>Sign
    in</button>';document.body.appendChild(f); then type anything into the password box and click Sign in. The
    full-page red card should stop the submit dead, naming the destination host.
 • Click Continue: the submit proceeds (httpbin will echo your field back), and a second submit of the same form is silent -
   the destination is now remembered as a salted hash.
 • Click Stop on a fresh hostile form: the layer dismisses, the page is untouched, and the status line reads "blocked a
   password to ...".
 • Flip Form Guardian off in the popup while the red card is showing: the card vanishes and the hostile form submits
   normally with no module present.

 Knob                                                  Where                              Default     Effect

 SSO_ACTION_ALLOWLIST                                  form-guardian.js                   7 hosts     Legit handoff origins

 CARD_AUTOCOMPLETE / CARD_NAME_RE                      form-guardian.js                   -           Card field recognition

 sl.approved cap                                       background/module-router.js        300         Memory list bound

 rememberApproved                                      settings.modules.formGuardian      true        Remembering on/off




SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                       69
   DEVIATION NOTE - Form Guardian intercepts native form submissions only. Credential exfiltration performed by page
   JavaScript through fetch/XHR is invisible to it - covering that would require request-body inspection, which MV3 makes
   impractical for store-listed extensions and which the design philosophy rejects anyway (we stop the classic phishing mechanic, the
   one that accounts for the overwhelming majority of real victims, and stay honest about the rest).



Part 5 - Ghost Click Blocker

5.1 Design analysis
The invisible overlay is the oldest trick in the annoyance playbook: a full-viewport div with opacity 0 (or 0.01) sits on top of
the content, so the ad network's click handler fires on *your* click and the button you aimed at never sees it. Ghost Click
Blocker's answer is deliberately surgical: when an element is provably a click-capturing ghost, the module sets
pointer-events: none on it - and that is *all* it does. It never removes the element, never touches the page's JavaScript,
never fakes clicks. The overlay still exists; it just cannot see your pointer anymore. This is also why the cleanup story is
perfect: deleting one inline style attribute restores the page bit-for-bit.

The ghost test is a conjunction of five conditions, all of which must hold, re-verified after a persistence window: computed
opacity below 0.15; actually laid out with nonzero size; covering at least 50% of the viewport; pointer-interactive
(pointer-events not already none) and on top of the main content; and *contentless* - no text, at most two child elements,
no form controls, no media. The contentless condition is what keeps video-player overlay divs (which are also large and
transparent-ish) out of the blast radius: player wrappers have real children and often real function. Candidates must persist in
this state for 600 ms before being neutralized, which filters load-time scaffolding that pages tear down themselves.

 Page element                                                  Ghost verdict         Why

 Full-viewport div, opacity 0.01, empty, stays 600 ms          neutralized           The textbook click thief

 Video player transparent control layer                        left alone            Has interactive children; contentless test fails

 Cookie overlay backdrop (dimmed, opacity 0.6)                 left alone            Opacity 0.6 fails the < 0.15 bar

 Sticky header that is transparent but has text                left alone            Contentless test fails

 Ad landing interstitial with an iframe                        left alone            iframes count as content

 Skeleton shimmer overlay during load (< 600 ms)               left alone            Persistence window not met


Chrome APIs used: none beyond the bridge - pure DOM and computed styles. Popup row: "Ghost Click Blocker - 2 ghosts
neutralized on this page", with a Show me action in Part 11's polished popup that flashes the neutralized elements (the module
listens for a flashOverlays message; the flash outline is temporary and removes itself). Options entries: annotate toggle
(default off - silent operation), viewport ratio slider (30-90%), persistence window (300-2000 ms). Toggle-off cleanup: every
neutralized element gets its inline pointer-events value restored and its data-sl-ghost attribute removed; observers
disconnect; the flash style rule if present is dropped; the script id unregisters.


5.2 Files
  modules/ghost-click/ghost-click.js
/* modules/ghost-click/ghost-click.js - neutralize invisible
 * click-capturing overlays with pointer-events:none. NEVER removes
 * elements, NEVER touches page JavaScript. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (!globalThis.SL || !SL.__bridge) return;

  /* All five conditions must hold simultaneously. */


SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                                70
  const OPACITY_MAX = 0.15;
  const VIEWPORT_RATIO_MIN = 0.5;
  const PERSIST_MS = 600;
  const MAX_CHILDREN = 2;
  const RESCAN_MS = 900;

  const neutralized = new Map();    // element -> saved inline pointer-events

  function computed(el) {
    return window.getComputedStyle(el);
  }

  function coversViewport(el, rect) {
    const vw = window.innerWidth, vh = window.innerHeight;
    if (!vw || !vh || !rect.width || !rect.height) return false;
    const covered = Math.min(rect.width, vw) * Math.min(rect.height, vh);
    return covered / (vw * vh) >= VIEWPORT_RATIO_MIN;
  }

  function isContentless(el) {
    if ((el.textContent || '').trim().length > 0) return false;
    if (el.childElementCount > MAX_CHILDREN) return false;
    // anything functional or media means this is page machinery
    return !el.querySelector(
      'input, select, textarea, button, a, video, audio, canvas, ' +
      'iframe, svg, img, label, form, object, embed');
  }

  function isGhost(el) {
    const style = computed(el);
    if (style.visibility === 'hidden' || style.display === 'none') {
      return false;                    // not participating in layout
    }
    if (parseFloat(style.opacity) > OPACITY_MAX) return false;
    if (style.pointerEvents === 'none') return false; // already inert
    const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (!rect.width || !rect.height) return false;
    if (!coversViewport(el, rect)) return false;
    if (parseInt(style.zIndex || '0', 10) < 0) return false;
    return isContentless(el);
  }

  function neutralize(el) {
    neutralized.set(el, el.style.pointerEvents);
    el.style.setProperty('pointer-events', 'none', 'important');
    el.setAttribute('data-sl-ghost', '1');
    if (cfg.annotate) el.classList.add('sl-ghost-flash');
    count = neutralized.size;
    SL.status('ghostClick', count + ' ghost' + (count === 1 ? '' : 's') +
               ' neutralized on this page');
  }

  function restore(el) {
    const saved = neutralized.get(el);
    if (typeof saved === 'string' && saved !== '') {
      el.style.pointerEvents = saved;
    } else {
      el.style.removeProperty('pointer-events');
    }
    el.removeAttribute('data-sl-ghost');
    el.classList.remove('sl-ghost-flash');
    neutralized.delete(el);
  }

  function restoreAll() {
    for (const el of Array.from(neutralized.keys())) {
      if (el.isConnected) restore(el);
      else neutralized.delete(el);
    }
  }

  /* Candidates must PERSIST as ghosts before we act. */
  let candidates = new Map();    // element -> first-seen timestamp
  let cfg = { annotate: false };



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                        71
  let count = 0;
  let scanTimer = 0;

  function scan() {
    const now = Date.now();
    const seen = new Set();
    for (const el of document.body ? document.body.querySelectorAll('*') : []) {
      const style = computed(el);
      if (style.position !== 'fixed' && style.position !== 'absolute') {
        continue;
      }
      if (!isGhost(el)) continue;
      seen.add(el);
      if (!candidates.has(el)) candidates.set(el, now);
      else if (now - candidates.get(el) >= PERSIST_MS &&
               !neutralized.has(el)) {
        neutralize(el);
      }
    }
    // forget candidates that stopped qualifying (page tore them down)
    for (const el of Array.from(candidates.keys())) {
      if (!seen.has(el) && !neutralized.has(el)) candidates.delete(el);
      if (!el.isConnected) { candidates.delete(el); neutralized.delete(el); }
    }
  }

  function schedule() {
    clearTimeout(scanTimer);
    scanTimer = setTimeout(() => { scan(); schedule(); }, RESCAN_MS);
  }

  /* Popup 'Show me': flash outlines for 2.5 seconds, then fade out. */
  SL.msg.listen('ghostClick', (msg) => {
    if (msg.type === 'flashOverlays') {
      for (const el of neutralized.keys()) {
        el.classList.add('sl-ghost-flash');
        setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('sl-ghost-flash'), 2500);
      }
    }
    return null;
  });

  function attach() {
    scan();
    schedule();
    SL.status('ghostClick', '0 ghosts neutralized on this page');
  }

  function detach() {
    clearTimeout(scanTimer);
    restoreAll();
    candidates = new Map();
    count = 0;
    SL.status('ghostClick', 'off');
  }

  SL.boot('ghostClick', {
    onEnabled(config) { cfg = Object.assign(cfg, config); attach(); },
    onConfig(next) { cfg = Object.assign(cfg, next || {}); },
    onDisabled() { detach(); }
  });
  SL.registerCleanup('ghostClick', detach);
})();

  modules/ghost-click/ghost-click.css
/* modules/ghost-click/ghost-click.css
 * .sl-ghost-flash lives in components.css; nothing else needed. */

5.4 Load & test
 • Reload the extension and confirm the registration log line.
 • Inject a textbook ghost into any page from DevTools, then try to click anything underneath it: var g=document.createE
    lement('div');g.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;background:#000;opacity:0.01;z-index:2147
    483647;';g.addEventListener('click',function(){console.log('GHOST CAPTURED YOUR
    CLICK')});document.body.appendChild(g); - within about a second (persistence + rescan cadence) the console stops
    printing captures and clicks reach the real page again.
 • Prove it is the module: flip Ghost Click Blocker off in the popup - the ghost instantly captures clicks again (its style is
   restored). Flip it back on and wait one rescan cycle.
 • Negative test - a *legitimate* transparent layer with content: var ok=document.createElement('div');ok.style.cs
    sText='position:fixed;inset:0;opacity:0.01;z-index:2147483646;';ok.innerHTML='<button>Real
    overlay button</button>';document.body.appendChild(ok); - it is never neutralized (contentless test fails), and
    its button remains clickable.
 • Check the popup status line ("1 ghost neutralized on this page"); in Part 11 the Show me button will flash these elements.


5.5 Tuning knobs
   Knob                                  Where                                  Default        Effect

   OPACITY_MAX                           ghost-click.js                         0.15           Opacity bar for ghost candidacy

   VIEWPORT_RATIO_MIN                    ghost-click.js                         0.5            Minimum viewport coverage

   PERSIST_MS                            ghost-click.js                         600            Time a ghost must persist

   MAX_CHILDREN                          ghost-click.js                         2              Contentless ceiling

   RESCAN_MS                             ghost-click.js                         900            Scan cadence

   annotate                              settings.modules.ghostClick            false          Persistent outline on ghosts



   TIP - The pointer-events: none approach is also the honest cleanup story: because the module's entire footprint is one inline
   style per ghost plus a data attribute, "OFF is real OFF" restores the page by deleting exactly what it added. If you are ever tempted
   to also delete ghost elements, resist - deletion is irreversible page surgery and breaks the guarantee.




SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                                   73
Part 6 - Fake Download Button

6.1 Design analysis
On a download site, the real button is the one that starts the file; the fake ones are decoys whose clicks pay someone else.
Humans distinguish them by gestalt - real buttons do not come in threes, do not float in corners, and do not live next to the
word "Advertisement". Fake Download Button encodes exactly that gestalt. It collects all download-worded interactive
elements on the page, then flags the ones that look like decoys: duplicates competing for the same verb, elements positioned
in ad zones (fixed/absolute corners, aside regions, within labelled advertisement containers), or links that do not actually point
at a file and lead cross-origin. Every flag is an annotation - amber dashed outline plus a hover pill with one plain sentence -
never a block; the module's contract is "mark the suspects," and Download Guard (Part 7) is the layer that inspects what
actually downloads.

The heuristic funnel keeps precision high on real pages. A candidate must be an a or button whose trimmed text matches the
download vocabulary (under 40 characters, so "Download the complete 90-page user manual" does not count). The page's
*primary* download is identified and protected: the candidate whose href most resembles a direct file (same-origin, or ending
in a known file extension) and that lives inside main content - it gets a green check instead of suspicion. Only when at least
two decoy-pattern candidates remain does the module annotate; a single ambiguous button is left alone, because the cost of a
wrong amber outline on a legit button is higher than the value of one caught decoy.

 Pattern                                                        Annotation                                Reasoning

 4+ 'Download' buttons scattered in corners/sidebars            amber on the decoys, green check on the   Competing buttons is itself the
                                                                primary                                   tell

 'Free Download' button inside a container labelled             amber                                     Explicit ad placement
 Advertisement/Sponsored

 Download button with javascript: or # href                     amber                                     Real downloads have
                                                                                                          destinations

 Direct same-origin link ending in .zip/.pdf/.exe/... in main   green check (primary)                     Looks like the actual file
 content

 Single download-ish button, ambiguous placement                none                                      One ambiguous button is not a
                                                                                                          signal

 Browsers' own download prompts, extension popups               none (not page DOM)                       Out of scope by construction


Chrome APIs used: none beyond the bridge. Popup row: "Fake Download Button - 2 decoy buttons marked". Options
entries: "mark the primary download too" (green check, on by default), decoy vocabulary is code-curated. Toggle-off
cleanup: outlines, attributes and hover pills removed from every marked element; scanner disconnected; script id
sl-cs-fake-download unregisters.



6.2 Files
  modules/fake-download/fake-download.js
/* modules/fake-download/fake-download.js - mark decoy download
 * buttons. Annotation only: never blocks, never hides. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (!globalThis.SL || !SL.__bridge) return;

  const TEXT_MAX = 40;
  const CLUSTER_MIN = 2;                      // decoys needed before annotating
  const SCAN_DELAY_MS = 800;
  const DOWNLOAD_WORDS = [



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                                  74
     'download', 'start download', 'free download', 'download now',
     'get it now', 'start now', 'direct download', 'click to download'
  ];
  const FILE_EXT_RE = /\.(zip|rar|7z|tar|gz|exe|msi|dmg|pkg|apk|iso|pdf|mp3|mp4|avi|mkv)(\?|$)/i;
  const AD_ZONE_RE = /(advertisement|sponsored|ads?|promo|banner)/i;

  let cfg = { markPrimary: true };
  let scanTimer = 0;
  const marked = [];

  function isCandidateText(text) {
    const t = (text || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!t || t.length > TEXT_MAX) return false;
    return DOWNLOAD_WORDS.some((w) => t === w || t.startsWith(w + ' ') ||
                                    t.indexOf(w) !== -1);
  }

  function candidates() {
    const out = [];
    for (const el of document.querySelectorAll('a, button')) {
      const text = el.textContent || el.getAttribute('value') || '';
      if (!isCandidateText(text)) continue;
      out.push({ el, text: text.trim() });
    }
    return out;
  }

  function inAdZone(el) {
    let node = el;
    for (let depth = 0; depth < 6 && node; depth++) {
      if (node.nodeType !== 1) { node = node.parentElement; continue; }
      const clue = (node.className && String(node.className)) + ' ' +
                   (node.id || '') + ' ' +
                   (node.getAttribute('aria-label') || '');
      if (AD_ZONE_RE.test(clue)) return true;
      if (node.tagName === 'ASIDE') return true;
      node = node.parentElement;
    }
    const style = window.getComputedStyle(el);
    return style.position === 'fixed';
  }

  function hrefOf(el) {
    if (el.tagName === 'A') return el.getAttribute('href') || '';
    return '';
  }

  function looksLikeRealFile(el) {
    const href = hrefOf(el);
    if (!href || /^javascript:|^#/i.test(href)) return false;
    try {
      const u = new URL(href, location.href);
      if (u.origin !== location.origin) return false;
      if (FILE_EXT_RE.test(u.pathname)) return true;
      return u.pathname.split('/').pop().indexOf('.') === -1 &&
             el.closest('main, article, #content, .content') !== null;
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }

  function annotate(el, note) {
    if (el.hasAttribute('data-sl-fake')) return;
    el.classList.add('sl-outline-warn');
    el.setAttribute('data-sl-fake', note);
    el.setAttribute('title', note);   // native tooltip as fallback
    el.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => pillFor(el, note));
    el.addEventListener('mouseleave', hidePill);
    marked.push(el);
  }

  let pillEl = null;
  function pillFor(el, note) {
    hidePill();
    pillEl = SLVerdict.pill('SECOND_LOOK', note);
    pillEl.classList.add('sl-ls-pill');



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                            75
     document.body.appendChild(pillEl);
     const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
     pillEl.style.left = Math.min(window.scrollX + r.left,
       window.scrollX + window.innerWidth - 260) + 'px';
     pillEl.style.top = (window.scrollY + r.bottom + 4) + 'px';
  }
  function hidePill() {
    if (pillEl) { pillEl.remove(); pillEl = null; }
  }

  function markPrimary(el) {
    if (!cfg.markPrimary || el.hasAttribute('data-sl-fake')) return;
    const check = SLVerdict.pill('CLEAR', 'looks like the real file');
    check.classList.add('sl-fd-primary');
    check.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;z-index:8000;';
    if (el.positionStatic) el.style.position = 'relative';
    try { el.appendChild(check); marked.push(check); } catch (e) {}
  }

  function scan() {
    if (!document.body) return;
    const cands = candidates();
    const decoys = [];
    let primary = null;
    for (const c of cands) {
      if (looksLikeRealFile(c.el) && !primary) { primary = c.el; continue; }
      if (inAdZone(c.el)) { decoys.push(c); continue; }
      const href = hrefOf(c.el);
      if (!href || /^javascript:|^#/i.test(href)) decoys.push(c);
    }
    // Competing verbs: everything except the primary is a suspect.
    if (cands.length >= CLUSTER_MIN + 1) {
      for (const c of cands) {
        if (c.el !== primary) decoys.push(c);
      }
    }
    if (primary) markPrimary(primary);
    const seen = new Set();
    for (const d of decoys) {
      if (seen.has(d.el)) continue;
      seen.add(d.el);
      const href = hrefOf(d.el);
      const note = cands.length >= CLUSTER_MIN + 1
        ? 'Several buttons compete for this download - real ones rarely do.'
        : 'This download button sits in an ad zone.';
      annotate(d.el, href ? note : note + ' Its link goes nowhere.');
    }
    SL.status('fakeDownload', decoys.length
      ? decoys.length + ' decoy button' +
        (decoys.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' marked'
      : 'No decoy download buttons');
  }

  function schedule() {
    clearTimeout(scanTimer);
    scanTimer = setTimeout(() => { scan(); schedule(); }, 2000);
  }

  function detach() {
    clearTimeout(scanTimer);
    hidePill();
    for (const el of marked) {
      if (el.classList) el.classList.remove('sl-outline-warn');
      if (el.removeAttribute) {
        el.removeAttribute('data-sl-fake');
        el.removeAttribute('title');
      }
      if (el.remove && el.classList &&
          el.classList.contains('sl-fd-primary')) el.remove();
    }
    marked.length = 0;
    SL.status('fakeDownload', 'off');
  }
  SL.boot('fakeDownload', {



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                       76
    onEnabled(config) { cfg = Object.assign(cfg, config); scan(); schedule(); },
    onConfig(next) { cfg = Object.assign(cfg, next || {}); },
    onDisabled() { detach(); }
  });
  SL.registerCleanup('fakeDownload', detach);
})();

  modules/fake-download/fake-download.css
/* modules/fake-download/fake-download.css
 * Outlines come from components.css (.sl-outline-warn). The hover pill
 * reuses .sl-ls-pill positioning from link-sniper.css, so both files
 * are injected together via the registry css array. */

              'modules/fake-download/fake-download.js'],

               'modules/link-sniper/link-sniper.css',
               'modules/fake-download/fake-download.css']

 • Reload the extension and confirm the new registration log.
 • The honest first test is a real download page - any software vendor's download page should produce zero amber outlines,
   and if it has an obvious primary file link, a small green "looks like the real file" pill attaches to it.
 • Build the decoy scenario in DevTools on any page: var
    ad=document.createElement('div');ad.className='advertisement';var
    a=document.createElement('a');a.href='https://elsewhere.example/x';a.textContent='Free
    Download';ad.appendChild(a);document.body.prepend(ad);var
    b=document.createElement('button');b.textContent='Download Now';b.style.position='fixed';b.styl
    e.right='10px';b.style.bottom='10px';document.body.appendChild(b); - within two seconds both decoys get
    amber dashed outlines, and hovering them shows the plain-language note.
 • Clicking a decoy still navigates (annotation, not blocking) - that is by design; if the decoy actually starts a download, Part 7
   is the layer that catches the file itself.
 • Flip the module off: outlines and pills vanish from every open tab; flip it on: they return after the next scan tick.

   TEXT_MAX                       fake-download.js                       40              Candidate text length ceiling

Part 7 - Download Guard

7.1 Design analysis
Download Guard is the file-safety pillar's single module, and it earns its interception rights with bytes rather than words: when
a download begins, the service worker fetches the first 16 bytes of the file with a Range header (host permissions make this
cross-origin legal) and compares them to a magic-number table. A file named invoice.pdf whose bytes begin with MZ is a
Windows executable wearing a PDF costume - that is a high-confidence contradiction, and it is the second and last situation in
which SecondLook will stop something from happening. Every stop ships with an undo: "Continue anyway" re-issues the
exact download through chrome.downloads.download.

The pipeline runs three checks in priority order, cheapest first. Filename forensics: double-extension disguises
(photo.jpg.exe, report.pdf.scr) and outright dangerous extensions (.scr, .pif, .hta, .bat, .cmd from the open web)
are flagged from the name alone, no network needed. Magic-byte sniffing: the mismatch test above, skipped for blob: and
data: URLs, which the service worker cannot fetch back (the honest fallback is filename analysis plus a status note, never a
guess). Everything else passes silently - ordinary files whose bytes match their extension category never produce so much as
a log entry, because a file checker that chatters about normal files trains users to ignore it.

 Situation                                                    Verdict                           Action

 Name ends .exe but bytes are %PDF                            SECOND LOOK note only             Contradiction is odd but harmless direction

 Name ends .pdf/.doc/.jpg/... but bytes are MZ or ELF         INTERCEPTED                       Download cancelled + undoable toast

 Double extension (.pdf.exe, .jpg.scr, ...)                   INTERCEPTED                       Cancelled before any bytes move

 .blob:/data: download with clean name                        silent                            Cannot sniff; honest fallback

 Archives (.zip) containing archives                          silent (this version)             See deviation note

 Extension and bytes agree                                    silent                            The boring majority, kept boring


Chrome APIs used: downloads (onCreated, cancel, erase, download), service-worker fetch with Range,
scripting.executeScript/insertCSS for the in-page undo toast, and tabs.query to locate the originating tab. Popup
row: "Download Guard - 1 file stopped this session" (per-tab status written directly by the SW). Options entries:
block-on-mismatch toggle (on), "always ask before executable downloads" is native Chrome behavior and deliberately not
duplicated. Toggle-off cleanup: no page footprint exists by default (the toast scripts are injected on demand, and the SW
listener early-exits when the module is off), so there is nothing to undo - which is the cleanest possible cleanup story.

   DEVIATION NOTE - chrome.downloads.DownloadItem carries no tab id, so the undo toast targets the tab whose URL
   matches the download's referrer, falling back to a toolbar badge alert if the referrer tab is gone or ambiguous. MV3 provides no
   better signal; this is documented rather than papered over.




7.2 Files




SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                                      78
  background/download-guard.js
/* background/download-guard.js - magic-byte mismatch interception.
 * SW-only module: no standing content script. */
(function (global) {
  'use strict';
  const root = global.SL = global.SL || {};
  if (root.DownloadGuard) return;

  const SNIFF_BYTES = 16;           // Range: bytes=0-15
  const DANGEROUS_EXT = /\.(scr|pif|hta|vbs|cpl|jar)$/i;
  const DISGUISE_RE = /\.(pdf|doc|docx|xls|xlsx|ppt|pptx|jpe?g|png|gif|txt|mp[34])(\.(exe|scr|bat|
  ↪ cmd|com|pif|vbs|js|jar|msi|dmg|apk))+$/i;
  const EXECUTABLE_MAGIC = [
     { bytes: [0x4d, 0x5a], label: 'Windows program (MZ)' },
     { bytes: [0x7f, 0x45, 0x4c, 0x46], label: 'Linux program (ELF)' }
  ];
  const DOCUMENT_MAGIC = [
     { bytes: [0x25, 0x50, 0x44, 0x46], label: 'PDF' },
     { bytes: [0x50, 0x4b, 0x03, 0x04], label: 'ZIP/Office' },
     { bytes: [0xd0, 0xcf, 0x11, 0xe0], label: 'legacy Office' },
     { bytes: [0xff, 0xd8, 0xff], label: 'JPEG' },
     { bytes: [0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47], label: 'PNG' }
  ];
  const DOC_EXT = /\.(pdf|docx?|xlsx?|pptx?|txt|jpe?g|png|gif|csv)$/i;

  let stoppedThisSession = 0;

  async function moduleOn(host) {
    const s = await SL.Settings.get();
    return SL.Settings.isModuleOn(s, 'downloadGuard', host || null);
  }

  function nameOf(item) {
    return (item.filename || item.url || '').split(/[\\/]/).pop() || '';
  }

  async function sniff(url) {
    // blob:/data: cannot be re-fetched by the SW - honest fallback.
    if (/^(blob|data):/i.test(url)) return { skipped: true };
    try {
      const res = await fetch(url, {
        headers: { Range: 'bytes=0-' + (SNIFF_BYTES - 1) },
        credentials: 'omit', cache: 'no-store'
      });
      const buf = new Uint8Array(await res.arrayBuffer());
      return { bytes: buf.slice(0, SNIFF_BYTES) };
    } catch (err) {
      return { skipped: true, error: String(err) };
    }
  }

  function startsWith(bytes, prefix) {
    if (!bytes) return false;
    for (let i = 0; i < prefix.length; i++) {
      if (bytes[i] !== prefix[i]) return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  async function findOriginTab(item) {
    const ref = item.referrer || item.finalUrl || item.url;
    try {
      const tabs = await chrome.tabs.query({ url: ref });
      if (tabs.length) return tabs[0];
      const host = new URL(ref).hostname;
      const fallback = await chrome.tabs.query({ url:
        'https://' + host + '/*' });
      return fallback[0] || null;
    } catch (e) { return null; }
  }

  async function notifyUser(item, reason, name) {
    const tab = await findOriginTab(item);



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                             79
      if (!tab) {
        await chrome.action.setBadgeText({ text: '!' });
        await chrome.action.setIcon({ path:
          '/assets/icons/icon-alert-48.png' });
        setTimeout(async () => {
          await chrome.action.setBadgeText({ text: '' });
          await chrome.action.setIcon({ path:
            '/assets/icons/icon-48.png' });
        }, 6000);
        return;
      }
      const target = { tabId: tab.id };
      try {
        await chrome.scripting.insertCSS({ target, files: [
          '/ui/tokens.css', '/ui/components.css'
        ] });
        await chrome.scripting.executeScript({ target, files: [
          '/shared/messaging.js',
          '/modules/download-guard/notify.js'
        ] });
        await chrome.tabs.sendMessage(tab.id, {
          module: 'downloadGuard',
          type: 'showBlocked',
          payload: { reason, name, url: item.finalUrl || item.url }
        });
      } catch (e) { /* tab not injectable (chrome:// etc.) - badge already
                       not an option; the download is still stopped */ }
  }

  async function stop(item, reason, name) {
    await chrome.downloads.cancel(item.id);
    await chrome.downloads.erase({ id: item.id });
    stoppedThisSession++;
    await SL.Storage.bumpStat('downloadGuard', 'INTERCEPTED');
    await SL.Storage.pushEvent({
      module: 'downloadGuard', verdict: 'INTERCEPTED',
      url: item.finalUrl || item.url, note: reason
    });
    const tabs = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true,
                                            currentWindow: true });
    if (tabs[0]) {
      const map = await SL.Storage.sessionGet('sl.status', {});
      map[tabs[0].id] = Object.assign({}, map[tabs[0].id]);
      map[tabs[0].id].downloadGuard = stoppedThisSession +
        ' file' + (stoppedThisSession === 1 ? '' : 's') +
        ' stopped this session';
      await SL.Storage.sessionSet('sl.status', map);
    }
    await notifyUser(item, reason, name);
  }

  async function onCreated(item) {
    try {
      const host = item.referrer ? new URL(item.referrer).hostname : null;
      if (!(await moduleOn(host))) return;    // settings gate: real OFF
      const name = nameOf(item);
      const settings = await SL.Settings.effective(host);
      const blockOnMismatch =
        settings.modules.downloadGuard.blockOnMismatch !== false;

        // 1. filename forensics - cheapest, no network
        if (DISGUISE_RE.test(name)) {
          await stop(item, 'The name hides a second extension - ' +
            'programs love to dress as documents.', name);
          return;
        }
        if (DANGEROUS_EXT.test(name)) {
          await stop(item, 'This file type is almost never legitimate ' +
            'when offered for download.', name);
          return;
        }

        // 2. magic-byte contradiction (documents claiming to be programs)
        if (blockOnMismatch && DOC_EXT.test(name)) {



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                     80
           const sniffed = await sniff(item.finalUrl || item.url);
           if (sniffed.skipped) return;
           for (const magic of EXECUTABLE_MAGIC) {
             if (startsWith(sniffed.bytes, magic.bytes)) {
               await stop(item, 'Named like a document, but its first ' +
                 'bytes are a ' + magic.label + '.', name);
               return;
             }
           }
        }
        // Bytes match category or nothing suspicious: silent pass.
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[sl:download-guard]', err);
      }
  }

  chrome.downloads.onCreated.addListener(onCreated);

  root.DownloadGuard = {
     onCreated, sniff, EXECUTABLE_MAGIC, DOCUMENT_MAGIC, DISGUISE_RE,
     DANGEROUS_EXT, DOC_EXT, SNIFF_BYTES
  };
})(globalThis);

  modules/download-guard/notify.js
/* modules/download-guard/notify.js - injected ON DEMAND into the tab
 * that started a blocked download. Shows the undoable toast. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (!globalThis.SL || globalThis.SL.__dgNotify) return;
  globalThis.SL.__dgNotify = true;

  SL.msg.listen('downloadGuard', (msg) => {
    if (msg.type !== 'showBlocked') return null;
    const p = msg.payload || {};
    SLToast.show({
      kind: 'danger',
      title: 'Download stopped: ' + (p.name || 'file'),
      body: p.reason || 'The file contradicted its own name.',
      timeoutMs: 0,
      actions: [
        { label: 'Undo - download it anyway', primary: true,
          onClick: () => SL.send('downloadGuard', 'downloadAgain',
                                 { url: p.url, filename: p.name }) },
        { label: 'Keep it stopped', onClick: () => {} }
      ]
    });
    return null;
  });
})();

7.4 Load & test
 • Reload the extension. Trigger an ordinary download (any file from any site) and confirm it completes with zero
   interference and zero console output.
 • The mismatch test needs a lying file. Easiest honest source: https://httpbin.org/bytes/64 named doc.pdf via a link
   you create in DevTools: var a=document.createElement('a');a.href='https://httpbin.org/bytes/64?x=1';
     a.setAttribute('download','report.pdf');a.textContent='Get report.pdf';document.body.prepend(a);
     then click it. Random bytes are not MZ, so this passes silently - correct behavior. To see the stop, serve a real MZ file: run
     the Part 12 test server (python3 test-pages/serve-tests.py) and click its "invoice.pdf (actually a program)" link -
     the download is cancelled instantly and the red toast appears in the page with the Undo button.
 • Double-extension test without any server: var b=document.createElement('a');b.href='https://httpbin.org/
     bytes/8';b.setAttribute('download','photo.jpg.exe');b.textContent='photo.jpg.exe';document.body
     .prepend(b); - the name alone triggers the stop and toast.

 • Click Undo - download it anyway on any stopped download: the file downloads normally, and the event log (Part 11
   dashboard) records that you overrode the stop.
 • Flip Download Guard off and retry the double-extension link: the download proceeds untouched; flip it back on to restore
   the guard.


7.5 Tuning knobs
 Knob                               Where                                   Default        Effect

 SNIFF_BYTES                        background/download-guard.js            16             Range length fetched

 DISGUISE_RE                        background/download-guard.js            see code       Double-extension matcher

 DANGEROUS_EXT                      background/download-guard.js            6 exts         Name-only stop list

 EXECUTABLE_MAGIC                   background/download-guard.js            MZ, ELF        Byte prefixes that contradict doc names

 blockOnMismatch                    settings.modules.downloadGuard          true           Sniffing on/off



     DEVIATION NOTE - Archive content inspection (a .zip whose contents are an .exe) is out of scope: MV3 service workers can
     fetch the archive but decompression-inspection of every download is a performance and false-positive minefield. The disguise
     patterns plus byte contradictions catch the overwhelming share of real-world attacks; the store listing says exactly that and no
     more.




SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                                82
Part 8 - Tab Guard

8.1 Design analysis
Popup storms are the denial-of-service attack against your attention: a page or its ad stack starts opening tab after tab, and by
the time you reach for the close button you have six new tabs, three of which have already loaded autoplaying media. Tab
Guard contains the storm in one move: it counts new tabs *by opener* inside a sliding window, and once one opener exceeds
the threshold within the window, every additional tab it spawns is diverted into a collapsed tab group named "Held for review"
- visible, countable, and silent, but not loading into your face. The group has a one-click Release that ungroups everything, and
the first two tabs of any burst are always allowed through so that legitimate multi-tab flows (a site legitimately opening a login
popup plus a callback tab) never get quarantined.

Containment, not blocking, is the deliberate design. Closing tabs that a page opened is destructive (one of them might be a
payment handoff you actually wanted), and Chrome's own popup blocker already handles the fully-automated case; Tab
Guard's value is the gray zone of *user-gesture-adjacent* storms that slip past it. A collapsed group neutralizes the storm's
cost - attention and noise - while keeping every tab recoverable, which is the same reversibility contract every other module
signs.

 Pattern                                               Behavior                                  Why

 A page opens its 1st and 2nd tabs in 10 s             allowed                                   Legitimate flows live here

 3rd+ tabs from the same opener within the window      moved to 'Held for review' group          The storm signature

 Storm tabs already in the group                       stay grouped                              No repeated moves, no flicker

 Login popup + callback tab (2 tabs)                   allowed                                   Under threshold

 Group released by the user                            all tabs ungrouped, window state reset    Explicit user intent

 Module toggled off mid-storm                          held tabs ungrouped automatically         OFF is real OFF


Chrome APIs used: tabs.onCreated (with openerTabId), tabGroups (create/update the isolation group),
tabs.group/tabs.ungroup, storage.session for the window state. Popup row: "Tab Guard - 3 tabs held in the storm
pen". Options entries: threshold (2-10, default 3), window length (5-60 s, default 10). Toggle-off cleanup: the isolation
group, if this module created one, is ungrouped - held tabs return to normal - and the session window state is deleted; the
listener (which lives in the SW) early-exits on every event thereafter.


8.2 Files
  background/tab-guard.js
/* background/tab-guard.js - popup-storm containment via tab groups.
 * SW-only module: no content script. */
(function (global) {
  'use strict';
  const root = global.SL = global.SL || {};
  if (root.TabGuard) return;

  const STATE_KEY = 'sl.tabguard';    // session: {openerId -> timestamps[]}
  const GROUP_TITLE = 'Held for review';
  let cfg = { threshold: 3, windowMs: 10000 };
  let groupId = null;                 // our tab group id (chrome.tabGroups)

  async function moduleOn() {
    const s = await SL.Settings.get();
    return SL.Settings.isModuleOn(s, 'tabGuard', null);
  }
  async function loadState() {



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                         83
      return SL.Storage.sessionGet(STATE_KEY, {});
  }

  async function saveState(state) {
    await SL.Storage.sessionSet(STATE_KEY, state);
  }

  function pruneTimestamps(stamps, now) {
    return (stamps || []).filter((t) => now - t < cfg.windowMs);
  }

  async function ensureGroup(windowId) {
    if (groupId !== null) return groupId;
    const created = await chrome.tabs.group({ createProperties: {
      windowId: windowId, titleState: 'EXPLICIT' }});
    groupId = created;
    try {
      await chrome.tabGroups.update(groupId, {
        title: GROUP_TITLE, collapsed: true, color: 'grey'
      });
    } catch (e) { /* tabGroups.update optional polish */ }
    return groupId;
  }

  async function releaseGroup() {
    if (groupId === null) return;
    const state = await loadState();
    try {
      const tabs = await chrome.tabs.query({ groupId });
      const ids = tabs.map((t) => t.id);
      if (ids.length) await chrome.tabs.ungroup(ids);
    } catch (e) { /* group already gone */ }
    groupId = null;
    await saveState({});
    SL.Storage.sessionSet(STATE_KEY, {});
  }

  async function onCreated(tab) {
    try {
      if (!(await moduleOn())) return;          // real OFF: early exit
      const openerId = tab.openerTabId;
      if (typeof openerId !== 'number') return; // user-opened tab
      let opener;
      try { opener = await chrome.tabs.get(openerId); }
      catch (e) { return; }                      // opener already closed
      if (!opener.url || !/^https?:/i.test(opener.url)) return;

        const now = Date.now();
        const state = await loadState();
        state[openerId] = pruneTimestamps(state[openerId], now);
        state[openerId].push(now);
        await saveState(state);

        const count = state[openerId].length;
        if (count < cfg.threshold) return;        // legit flow so far

        // Storm confirmed: divert this and every further tab.
        const g = await ensureGroup(tab.windowId);
        try {
          await chrome.tabs.group({ tabIds: [tab.id], groupId: g });
        } catch (e) { /* tab navigated away already - acceptable */ }
        await writeStatus(openerId, count);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[sl:tab-guard]', err);
      }
  }

  async function writeStatus(openerId, count) {
    let host = '';
    try { host = (await chrome.tabs.get(openerId)).url &&
      new URL((await chrome.tabs.get(openerId)).url).hostname; }
    catch (e) {}
    const map = await SL.Storage.sessionGet('sl.status', {});
    // Attribute the status line to the opener tab - that is the storm.



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                    84
      map[openerId] = Object.assign({}, map[openerId]);
      map[openerId].tabGuard = count + ' tabs held from ' +
        (host || 'this page');
      await SL.Storage.sessionSet('sl.status', map);
  }

  async function onConfig(next) {
    cfg = Object.assign(cfg, next || {});
  }

  async function onDisabled() {
    await releaseGroup();
  }

  /* Popup / options control surface. */
  root.Router.register('tabGuard', {
    async releaseGroup() {
       await releaseGroup();
       return { released: true };
    },
    async heldCount() {
       if (groupId === null) return { count: 0 };
       const tabs = await chrome.tabs.query({ groupId });
       return { count: tabs.length, groupId };
    }
  });

  chrome.tabs.onCreated.addListener(onCreated);
  SL.Settings.subscribe(async (next) => {
    const on = SL.Settings.isModuleOn(next, 'tabGuard', null);
    const effective = (await SL.Settings.effective(null)).modules.tabGuard;
    Object.assign(cfg, effective);
    if (!on) await onDisabled();
  });

  root.TabGuard = { onCreated, releaseGroup, GROUP_TITLE };
})(globalThis);

  background/service-worker.js - importScripts list gains one line
  '/background/download-guard.js',              // <- Part 7
  '/background/tab-guard.js'                    // <- Part 8
);
