 • Reload the extension; the importScripts list now loads tab-guard.js (check the SW console for syntax errors on load - a
   blank console is success).
 • Open any page and run a controlled storm from its DevTools console: for (var i = 0; i < 6; i++)
   window.open(location.href, '_blank'); - the first two tabs open normally; from the third onward they land in a
   collapsed grey group titled "Held for review" at the top of your tab strip.
 • Click the group to expand it: all held tabs are there, intact and recoverable.
 • From the popup, Part 11 wires a Release button; for now release from the SW console:
   SL.Router.HANDLERS.tabGuard.releaseGroup[0]() is awkward to type, so simply drag tabs out of the group




SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                     85
    manually or flip the module off - which ungroups everything automatically. That off-switch release is itself the cleanup
    test: flip Tab Guard off and watch the group dissolve.
 • Confirm the two-tab allowance: window.open(location.href,'_blank');
   window.open(location.href,'_blank'); - both open ungrouped, no matter how quickly.



8.5 Tuning knobs
   Knob                       Where                          Default                    Effect

   threshold                  settings.modules.tabGuard      3                          Tabs per opener before containment

   windowMs                   settings.modules.tabGuard      10000                      Sliding window length

   GROUP_TITLE                background/tab-guard.js        'Held for review'          Isolation group name

   STATE_KEY                  background/tab-guard.js        'sl.tabguard'              Session state key



   WARNING - The sliding-window state lives in chrome.storage.session, which MV3 clears when the browser exits -
   exactly the lifecycle a popup-storm counter should have. Do not move it to storage.local: a stale count from yesterday's
   session quarantining today's first tab is a bug no user will forgive.



Part 9 - Cookie Tamer

9.1 Design analysis
Consent banners are a negotiation you did not ask to hold, usually designed so that "Accept All" is a big blue button and
"Reject" is a grey text link two dialogs deep. Cookie Tamer's policy is the one that makes it a privacy tool rather than an
auto-clicker: it never clicks Accept, ever. When it finds a consent banner it does one of two things. If the banner exposes an
honest rejection control - a button whose own text says reject, refuse, decline, or "necessary cookies only" - Cookie Tamer
clicks exactly that button, once, and verifies the banner actually left the DOM. If there is no honest rejection control, or the
button does not dismiss the banner, the module *parks* the banner: it is hidden (reversibly) and replaced by a slim bar of our
own saying the banner is parked, with a "Decide now" button that restores the original, scrolls to it, and steps aside so you can
make the choice yourself.

The button classifier is the safety-critical piece, so it is deliberately paranoid. A button qualifies as a rejection only when its
accessible text (plus aria-label and title) matches the rejection vocabulary and does *not* contain any accept-flavored word -
"Accept necessary" passes the first test but contains "accept", so it is rejected by the second and the banner falls back to
parking. Short affirmative labels like "OK" and "Got it" are classified as neither reject nor accept-safe: they are exactly the
buttons consent dark patterns want automated clickers to press, so they never receive a click from us. Parking, not clicking, is
always the safe default.

 Banner situation                                                Action                                Rationale

 OneTrust banner with a visible 'Reject All' control             one click on Reject, verify removal   The good case: honest control

 Banner whose only controls are 'Accept All' and 'OK'            park + slim bar                       No honest reject exists

 'Reject' button that fails to dismiss the banner                park + slim bar                       Never click twice, never guess

 Dark pattern: reject hidden behind 'Manage preferences'         park + slim bar                       We do not walk multi-step mazes
 sub-dialog

 No banner detected                                              silent                                Nothing to negotiate




SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                                86
 Banner situation                                             Action                             Rationale

 Login flow on the same site later                            untouched                          We only act on consent banners


The second capability is the site sweep, a deliberate, user-invoked action (never automatic, never scheduled): from the popup
or dashboard you can sweep the current site's cookies down to the functional set. Login and session cookies are preserved by
name heuristics (SID, SAPISID, __Secure-*, session, auth, csrf, ...) and by the structural rule that any HttpOnly +
Secure cookie is almost certainly an authentication token and survives by default; analytics and ad cookies do not. Chrome
APIs used: content-side none beyond the bridge; sweep-side cookies in the service worker. Popup row: "Cookie Tamer - 1
banner parked" / "standing by". Options entries: strategy (prefer-reject / park-only), sweep is exposed as a button in Part 11's
popup. Toggle-off cleanup: parked banners are restored to exactly their prior inline styles, the slim bar is removed, observers
detach, script id sl-cs-cookie-tamer unregisters.


9.2 Files
  modules/cookie-tamer/cookie-tamer.js
/* modules/cookie-tamer/cookie-tamer.js - consent banner tamer.
 * POLICY: never clicks Accept. Clicks only an explicit reject control;
 * otherwise parks the banner behind a slim, reversible bar. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (!globalThis.SL || !SL.__bridge) return;

  /* CMP fingerprints: one selector per provider family. */
  const CMP_SELECTORS = [
     '#onetrust-consent-sdk',           // OneTrust
     '#CybotCookieDialog',              // CookieBot
     'div[id^="didomi"], span[id^="didomi"]', // Didomi
     '#cmpbox', '#cmpwrapper',          // consentmanager
     '#cookie-law-info-bar',           // CookieYes / GDPR-era WP plugins
     '[data-testid="cookie-banner"]',   // React apps
     '[class*="cookie-consent"]',
     '[id*="cookieBanner"]'
  ];
  const GENERIC_RE = /cookie|consent/i;

  const REJECT_RE = /reject all|reject|refuse|decline|decline all|necessary (cookies
  ↪ )?only|essential (cookies )?only|deny|opt[- ]?out/i;
  const ACCEPT_DANGER_RE = /accept all|accept|agree|allow all|consent|allow|enable/i;
  const AMBIGUOUS_OK_RE = /^(ok|okay|got it|gotcha|continue|close|dismiss|x|\u2715)$/i;

  const PARK_BAR_TEXT = 'Cookie banner parked - it is hidden, not answered.';

  let cfg = { strategy: 'prefer-reject' };
  let parked = [];      // {banner, savedStyle, barEl}
  let scanTimer = 0;
  let observer = null;

  function findBanner() {
    for (const sel of CMP_SELECTORS) {
      try {
        const el = document.querySelector(sel);
        if (el && el.offsetHeight > 0) return el;
      } catch (e) { /* bad selector - skip */ }
    }
    // Generic fallback: fixed/absolute overlay mentioning cookies.
    for (const el of document.querySelectorAll(
      'div, section, aside, dialog')) {
      const style = window.getComputedStyle(el);
      if (style.position !== 'fixed' && style.position !== 'absolute') {
        continue;
      }
      const text = (el.id + ' ' + (el.className && String(el.className)) +
        ' ' + el.textContent.slice(0, 300)).trim();
      if (GENERIC_RE.test(text) && el.offsetHeight > 40 &&
          el.querySelectorAll('button, a[role="button"], input').length) {



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                          87
            return el;
        }
      }
      return null;
  }

  function buttonLabels(banner) {
    const out = [];
    for (const b of banner.querySelectorAll(
      'button, a[role="button"], input[type="button"], input[type="submit"]')) {
      const label = ((b.getAttribute('aria-label') || '') + ' ' +
        (b.getAttribute('title') || '') + ' ' +
        (b.value || '') + ' ' + (b.textContent || '')).trim();
      if (label) out.push({ el: b, label: label.slice(0, 120) });
    }
    return out;
  }

  function findRejectButton(banner) {
    for (const { el, label } of buttonLabels(banner)) {
      if (AMBIGUOUS_OK_RE.test(label.trim())) continue;   // never 'OK'
      if (ACCEPT_DANGER_RE.test(label)) continue;          // never accept
      if (REJECT_RE.test(label)) return el;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function gone(el) {
    return !el || !el.isConnected || el.offsetHeight === 0;
  }

  async function tryReject(banner) {
    if (cfg.strategy !== 'prefer-reject') return false;
    const btn = findRejectButton(banner);
    if (!btn) return false;
    btn.click();                       // exactly one click, ever
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 450));
    if (gone(banner)) {
      SL.status('cookieTamer', 'rejected via the honest button');
      await SL.Storage.pushEvent({
        module: 'cookieTamer', verdict: null, url: location.href,
        note: 'clicked explicit reject control'
      });
      return true;
    }
    return false;                      // did not dismiss: park instead
  }

  function park(banner) {
    if (parked.some((p) => p.banner === banner)) return;
    const saved = banner.getAttribute('style');
    banner.style.setProperty('display', 'none', 'important');
    const bar = document.createElement('div');
    bar.className = 'sl-banner sl-root';
    const text = document.createElement('span');
    text.className = 'sl-banner__text';
    text.textContent = PARK_BAR_TEXT;
    const decide = document.createElement('button');
    decide.className = 'sl-btn';
    decide.type = 'button';
    decide.textContent = 'Decide now';
    decide.addEventListener('click', () => {
      unpark(banner);
      banner.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center',
        behavior: (window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)')
          .matches ? 'auto' : 'smooth') });
    });
    const keep = document.createElement('button');
    keep.className = 'sl-btn';
    keep.type = 'button';
    keep.textContent = 'Keep hidden';
    keep.addEventListener('click', () => bar.remove());
    bar.appendChild(text);
    bar.appendChild(decide);



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                           88
      bar.appendChild(keep);
      (document.body || document.documentElement).appendChild(bar);
      parked.push({ banner, saved, barEl: bar });
      SL.status('cookieTamer', '1 banner parked (Accept was never clicked)');
  }

  function unpark(banner) {
    const idx = parked.findIndex((p) => p.banner === banner);
    if (idx === -1) return;
    const { banner: el, saved, barEl } = parked[idx];
    parked.splice(idx, 1);
    if (saved === null) el.removeAttribute('style');
    else el.setAttribute('style', saved);
    if (barEl) barEl.remove();
  }

  function unparkAll() {
    for (const { banner } of parked.slice()) unpark(banner);
  }

  async function scan() {
    if (!document.body) return;
    const banner = findBanner();
    if (!banner) return;
    const ok = await tryReject(banner);
    if (!ok && !gone(banner)) park(banner);
  }

  function schedule() {
    clearTimeout(scanTimer);
    scanTimer = setTimeout(() => { scan(); schedule(); }, 2500);
  }

  function attach() {
    scan();
    schedule();
    observer = new MutationObserver(() => { clearTimeout(scanTimer);
      scanTimer = setTimeout(scan, 350); });
    observer.observe(document.documentElement || document.body, {
      childList: true, subtree: true
    });
    SL.status('cookieTamer', 'standing by');
  }

  function detach() {
    clearTimeout(scanTimer);
    if (observer) { observer.disconnect(); observer = null; }
    unparkAll();
    SL.status('cookieTamer', 'off');
  }

  SL.boot('cookieTamer', {
    onEnabled(config) { cfg = Object.assign(cfg, config); attach(); },
    onConfig(next) { cfg = Object.assign(cfg, next || {}); },
    onDisabled() { detach(); }
  });
  SL.registerCleanup('cookieTamer', detach);
})();

  modules/cookie-tamer/cookie-tamer.css
/* modules/cookie-tamer/cookie-tamer.css
 * The parked bar uses .sl-banner from components.css. Nothing extra. */



9.3 Sweep route + registry entry
The cookie sweep runs in the service worker, where chrome.cookies lives. Paste the route block into
background/module-router.js after the downloadGuard routes. Preservation is doubly conservative: cookies whose
names match the login vocabulary survive, and *any* cookie that is both HttpOnly and Secure survives regardless of name,
because that combination is the signature of an authentication token set by the server.



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                              89
  background/module-router.js - add after downloadGuard routes
  /* Cookie Tamer: user-invoked sweep of the current site's cookies.
   * Login/session cookies survive by name AND by structure. */
  const LOGIN_COOKIE_RE = /^(sid|hsid|ssid|sapisid|apisid|lsid|__secure-.*|.*session.*|.*login.*|.
  ↪ *auth.*|.*csrf.*|.*xsrf.*|.*remember.*)$/i;
  register('cookieTamer', {
    async sweepCurrentSite(payload) {
      const tab = await chrome.tabs.get(payload.tabId);
      const host = new URL(tab.url).hostname;
      const all = await chrome.cookies.getAll({ domain: host });
      let kept = 0, removed = 0;
      for (const c of all) {
        const keep = LOGIN_COOKIE_RE.test(c.name) ||
          (c.httpOnly && c.secure);
        if (keep) { kept++; continue; }
        const url = (c.secure ? 'https://' : 'http://') +
          (c.domain.replace(/^\./, '')) + c.path;
        try {
          await chrome.cookies.remove({ url, name: c.name });
          removed++;
        } catch (e) { /* cookie already gone */ }
      }
      await SL.Storage.pushEvent({
        module: 'cookieTamer', verdict: null, url: tab.url,
        note: 'sweep: kept ' + kept + ', removed ' + removed
      });
      return { kept, removed };
    }
  });

  shared/module-registry.js - Cookie Tamer entry (replace placeholder)
     { id: 'cookieTamer', name: 'Cookie Tamer', pillar: 'annoyance',
       blurb: 'Parks consent banners; never clicks Accept.',
       defaultEnabled: true,
       scripts: [{
         id: 'sl-cs-cookie-tamer', matches: ['http://*/*', 'https://*/*'],
         runAt: 'document_idle', world: 'ISOLATED', allFrames: false,
         js: ['shared/messaging.js', 'shared/cs-bridge.js',
              'ui/toast.js', 'ui/verdict-card.js',
              'modules/cookie-tamer/cookie-tamer.js'],
         css: ['ui/tokens.css', 'ui/components.css',
               'modules/cookie-tamer/cookie-tamer.css']
       }] },



9.4 Load & test
 • Reload the extension; confirm sl-cs-cookie-tamer registered.
 • Visit two or three mainstream news sites. On those with OneTrust or CookieBot banners carrying visible "Reject All"
   controls, the banner should dismiss itself within a couple of seconds (one click, the honest one). On banners with only
   accept-flavored controls, the slim grey bar appears at the bottom instead.
 • Click Decide now on the parked bar: the original banner reappears and scrolls into view, exactly as it was before parking.
 • Test the sweep from the SW console against the active tab: const t = (await
    chrome.tabs.query({active:true,currentWindow:true}))[0];
    SL.Router.HANDLERS.cookieTamer.sweepCurrentSite[0]({tabId: t.id}) - expect {kept: N, removed: M}
    with your login cookies among the kept.
 • Flip Cookie Tamer off while a banner is parked: the original banner reappears on every open tab (styles restored
   verbatim). Flip it on: the next scan parks it again.


9.5 Tuning knobs




SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                     90
   Knob                               Where                                 Default                  Effect

   CMP_SELECTORS                      cookie-tamer.js                       9 selectors              Provider fingerprints

   REJECT_RE                          cookie-tamer.js                       see code                 Honest-reject vocabulary

   ACCEPT_DANGER_RE                   cookie-tamer.js                       see code                 Veto vocabulary (never click)

   AMBIGUOUS_OK_RE                    cookie-tamer.js                       7 labels                 'OK'-class buttons never clicked

   strategy                           settings.modules.cookieTamer          'prefer-reject'          Click rejects or park only

   LOGIN_COOKIE_RE                    module-router.js                      see code                 Sweep preservation names



   WARNING - The classifier's ordering is load-bearing: the accept-veto runs BEFORE the reject-match, so a button labeled
   "Accept necessary cookies only" is never clicked even though it contains "necessary only". If you extend either vocabulary, re-test
   that ordering with a banner containing such hybrid labels - this is the single line of defense between a privacy tool and an
   auto-consent machine.



Part 10 - Webcam/Mic Indicator

10.1 Design analysis
When a page uses your camera or microphone, Chrome already tells you - a tiny camera glyph on the tab itself. The problem
is that the tab strip is the last place a focused user is looking. The indicator module makes the fact unmissable: from the
moment a page's getUserMedia or getDisplayMedia succeeds until the last track ends, a chip sits top-right of the page - red
pulsing dot, "Camera active" - and the toolbar icon for that tab switches to the camera variant with a REC badge. When the
last track ends, the chip leaves without a trace.

The engineering is the interesting part, because page JavaScript cannot be observed from the isolated world:
navigator.mediaDevices in the page's own world is a different object from the one content scripts see. The module
therefore ships its only MAIN-world script, injected at document_start before any page code runs, which wraps
getUserMedia and getDisplayMedia and reports activity across the world boundary via window.postMessage
(postMessage events are visible to both worlds; the payload carries a source tag the isolated bridge verifies). The bridge never
trusts the message beyond "which kind started or stopped" - no page data flows through it, only the words camera, mic and
screen.

 Event                                                               Chip                                              Toolbar badge

 Page calls getUserMedia({video:true}) and it resolves               'Camera active' + red pulse                       REC + camera icon

 getUserMedia({audio:true, video:true})                              'Camera + microphone active'                      REC

 getDisplayMedia (screen share)                                      'Screen sharing active'                           REC

 Last track ends (page stops or user revokes)                        chip removed                                      cleared

 Toggle module off mid-session                                       chip removed, future pages get no hook            cleared

 Page already had permission granted before this module loaded       nothing detectable (see note)                     -


Chrome APIs used: content-side none beyond the bridge; the MAIN-world file uses only native page APIs; the badge uses
chrome.action.setBadgeText/setIcon per tab via a router route. Popup row: "Webcam/Mic Indicator - watching" /
"CAMERA ACTIVE" when live. Options entries: chip position (top-right default), "also show while loading" (off - only
granted streams). Toggle-off cleanup: the chip and badge clear immediately, and future pages never receive the hook; pages
loaded while the module was on keep their (now unobserved) wrapper until reload - an honest limitation spelled out below.


SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                                   91
   DEVIATION NOTE - Two limitations are inherent and stated up front. First, camera/microphone use that began *before* this
   module loaded cannot be detected: the hook only sees calls made after injection (document_start of each navigation). Second,
   toggling the module off cannot un-hook the wrapper in already-loaded pages - the chip and badge go away, the page's
   getUserMedia wrapper remains inert until the page reloads. Both are consequences of how MAIN-world injection works in
   MV3, and both are documented in the README rather than hidden.




10.2 Files
  modules/av-indicator/main-world.js
/* modules/av-indicator/main-world.js - runs in the page's MAIN world
 * at document_start, BEFORE any page script. Wraps getUserMedia and
 * getDisplayMedia, reports starts/stops via window.postMessage.
 * SECURITY: no page data crosses the boundary - only the words
 * camera / mic / screen and start / stop. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (globalThis.__slAVHooked) return;
  globalThis.__slAVHooked = true;

  const TAG = 'secondlook-av';

  function post(event, media) {
    try {
      window.postMessage({ source: TAG, event: event, media: media },
                         location.origin);
    } catch (e) { /* cross-origin about:blank edge - ignore */ }
  }

  function kindsFromConstraints(constraints) {
    const kinds = [];
    const c = constraints || {};
    if (c.video !== undefined && c.video !== false) kinds.push('camera');
    if (c.audio !== undefined && c.audio !== false) kinds.push('mic');
    return kinds;
  }

  function watchTracks(stream, kinds) {
    for (const track of stream.getTracks()) {
      const kind = track.kind === 'video'
        ? (kinds.includes('screen') ? 'screen' : 'camera') : 'mic';
      track.addEventListener('ended', () => post('stop', [kind]));
      // 'inactive' on the MediaStream itself means all tracks ended
    }
    stream.addEventListener('inactive', () => {
      for (const k of kinds) post('stop', [k]);
    });
  }

  const devices = navigator.mediaDevices;
  if (devices && devices.getUserMedia) {
    const origGUM = devices.getUserMedia.bind(devices);
    devices.getUserMedia = async function (constraints) {
       const stream = await origGUM(constraints);
       try {
         const kinds = kindsFromConstraints(constraints);
         post('start', kinds);
         watchTracks(stream, kinds);
       } catch (e) { /* never break the page on our accounting */ }
       return stream;
    };
  }

  if (devices && devices.getDisplayMedia) {
    const origGDM = devices.getDisplayMedia.bind(devices);
    devices.getDisplayMedia = async function (constraints) {
      const stream = await origGDM(constraints);
      try {
        post('start', ['screen']);
        watchTracks(stream, ['screen']);


SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                          92
        } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
        return stream;
    };
  }
})();

  modules/av-indicator/indicator.js
/* modules/av-indicator/indicator.js - ISOLATED world bridge.
 * Listens for the MAIN world's postMessage reports, renders the chip,
 * relays state to the service worker for the toolbar badge. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (!globalThis.SL || !SL.__bridge) return;
  const TAG = 'secondlook-av';

  const active = new Set();       // 'camera' | 'mic' | 'screen'
  let chip = null;

  const LABELS = {
     camera: 'Camera', mic: 'microphone', screen: 'screen sharing'
  };

  function labelFor() {
    const parts = Array.from(active).map((k) => LABELS[k]);
    if (!parts.length) return '';
    return parts.length === 1 ? parts[0] + ' active'
      : parts.slice(0, -1).join(', ') + ' and ' +
        parts[parts.length - 1] + ' active';
  }

  function render() {
    if (!active.size) { removeChip(); return; }
    if (!chip) {
      chip = document.createElement('button');
      chip.type = 'button';
      chip.className = 'sl-chip sl-av-chip';
      const dot = document.createElement('span');
      dot.className = 'sl-chip__dot';
      chip.appendChild(dot);
      const text = document.createElement('span');
      text.className = 'sl-av-chip__text';
      chip.appendChild(text);
      chip.addEventListener('click', openCard);
      const mount = () => (document.body || document.documentElement)
        .appendChild(chip);
      if (document.body) mount();
      else document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', mount, { once: true });
    }
    chip.querySelector('.sl-av-chip__text').textContent = labelFor();
    SL.status('avIndicator', labelFor().toUpperCase());
  }

  function removeChip() {
    if (chip) { chip.remove(); chip = null; }
    SL.status('avIndicator', 'watching');
  }

  function openCard() {
    SLVerdict.card({
      verdict: 'SECOND_LOOK',
      title: 'This page is using your hardware',
      reasons: [labelFor() + '. You can stop it from the camera icon ' +
        'in Chrome\'s address bar.'],
      rows: [
        { label: 'Page', value: location.hostname },
        { label: 'In use', value: labelFor() }
      ]
    });
  }

  /* Only tagged messages from our MAIN-world wrapper are accepted. */
  window.addEventListener('message', (e) => {
    if (e.source !== window) return;



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                           93
    const data = e.data;
    if (!data || data.source !== TAG) return;
    if (data.event === 'start') {
      for (const k of data.media || []) active.add(k);
    } else if (data.event === 'stop') {
      for (const k of data.media || []) active.delete(k);
    }
    render();
    SL.send('avIndicator', 'activeChange',
            { active: active.size > 0, kinds: Array.from(active) });
  });

  SL.boot('avIndicator', {
    onEnabled() {
       SL.status('avIndicator', 'watching');
    },
    onDisabled() { removeChip(); }
  });
  SL.registerCleanup('avIndicator', removeChip);
})();

/* modules/av-indicator/av-indicator.css */
.sl-av-chip {
  border: 0;
  cursor: pointer;
  color: var(--sl-ink);
}
.sl-av-chip__text { font-weight: 600; }



10.3 Badge route + registry entry
The badge route lives in the router (paste after the cookieTamer block). Per-tab badges keep state naturally: Chrome scopes
setBadgeText({tabId}) to one tab, and the session map prunes itself on stops.

  background/module-router.js - add after cookieTamer routes
  register('avIndicator', {
    async activeChange(payload, sender) {
      const tabId = sender && sender.tab && sender.tab.id;
      if (typeof tabId !== 'number') return { ok: true };
      const map = await SL.Storage.sessionGet('sl.avtabs', {});
      if (payload.active) map[tabId] = true;
      else delete map[tabId];
      await SL.Storage.sessionSet('sl.avtabs', map);
      await chrome.action.setBadgeText({
        tabId, text: payload.active ? 'REC' : ''
      });
      if (payload.active) {
        await chrome.action.setIcon({
          tabId, path: '/assets/icons/icon-camera-48.png'
        });
      } else {
        await chrome.action.setIcon({
          tabId, path: '/assets/icons/icon-48.png'
        });
      }
      return { tabId, active: !!payload.active };
    }
  });

         {    // 1) MAIN world hook, before any page script runs
            id: 'sl-cs-av-main', matches: ['http://*/*', 'https://*/*'],

10.4 Load & test
 • Reload the extension; the registration log should now list both sl-cs-av-main and sl-cs-av-bridge.
 • Open any page that uses camera/mic demos (a "WebRTC sample" search finds plenty;
   https://webrtc.github.io/samples/ is the canonical one) and click its "Start" button. Grant permission: the
   red-pulse chip appears top-right with "Camera active", and this extension's toolbar icon for that tab shows the camera
   variant with a REC badge.
 • Click the page's "Stop" button: the chip disappears and the badge clears. If the page merely pauses the track rather than
   ending it, the chip correctly persists - only a real ended event clears it.
 • Screen-share test with getDisplayMedia: the chip reads "screen sharing active".
 • Click the chip: the card names the hardware in use and points to Chrome's own camera icon as the kill switch.
 • Flip the module off while the chip is showing: the chip and badge vanish immediately. Reload the page and use the camera
   again: no chip (the hook never injected), which is the real-OFF guarantee for future loads.


10.5 Tuning knobs
   Knob                          Where                                    Default                   Effect

   TAG                           main-world.js / indicator.js             'secondlook-av'           Cross-world message tag

   LABELS                        indicator.js                             3 entries                 Chip wording per kind

   postMessage origin            main-world.js                            location.origin           Boundary strictness



   WARNING - The MAIN-world wrapper must never alter call semantics: it returns the original promise and never rejects on its
   own, and all of its own accounting is wrapped in try/catch so a failure inside the wrapper can never break the page's media call. If
   you ever extend this file, that invariant is the review gate.



Part 11 - Options, Dashboard & Popup Polish

11.1 Design analysis
Part 11 turns the settings surface from "the popup's ten toggles" into a real control room, and it exists to keep a promise made
in every module part: that the knobs described there would be wired somewhere a user can reach. The options page is a
two-pane layout - navigation on the left, one pane per concern on the right - with a single hard rule inherited from Part 0:
pages read settings directly but never write them. Every control routes its change through the router
(core/setModuleOption, core/setSiteOverride, ...), which keeps the service worker the single writer and the toggle
lifecycle intact even when the edit comes from a full page instead of the popup.

The dashboard is the read-side counterpart: stat tiles for the three verdict counters, a module activity grid, the capped event
log rendered as a table (hostnames only - the Part 0 privacy boundary), and the site-override manager where per-site


SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                                  95
exceptions can be reviewed and lifted. The popup gets its final polish here: the Dashboard button wakes up, the site card gains
"Show ghosts" (flashes neutralized overlays via a new router route) and "Sweep cookies" (the Part 9 sweep, user-invoked), and
the chrome:// empty state stops pretending there is something to do.


11.2 Options page

  ui/options.js
/* ui/options.js - declarative settings UI. Reads storage directly,
 * WRITES ONLY THROUGH THE ROUTER (single-writer rule). */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  const send = SL.msg.send;
  const main = document.getElementById('slo-main');
  let settings = null;

  async function refresh() { settings = await SL.Settings.get(); }

  function el(tag, cls, text) {



SecondLook - Build Guide                                              97
      const n = document.createElement(tag);
      if (cls) n.className = cls;
      if (text !== undefined) n.textContent = text;
      return n;
  }

  /* ---- field builders (all write via router) ---------------------- */
  function toggleField(name, hint, module, get, set) {
    const row = el('div', 'slo-field');
    const label = el('div', 'slo-field__label');
    label.appendChild(el('div', 'slo-field__name', name));
    if (hint) label.appendChild(el('div', 'slo-field__hint', hint));
    const wrap = el('label', 'sl-toggle');
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'checkbox';
    input.checked = get();
    input.addEventListener('change', () => set(input.checked));
    const track = el('span', 'sl-toggle__track');
    wrap.appendChild(input); wrap.appendChild(track);
    row.appendChild(label); row.appendChild(wrap);
    return row;
  }

  function rangeField(name, hint, module, option, min, max, step, value) {
    const row = el('div', 'slo-field');
    const label = el('div', 'slo-field__label');
    label.appendChild(el('div', 'slo-field__name', name));
    if (hint) label.appendChild(el('div', 'slo-field__hint', hint));
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'range';
    input.min = String(min); input.max = String(max);
    input.step = String(step || 1); input.value = String(value);
    const out = el('output', null, String(value));
    input.addEventListener('input', () => { out.textContent = input.value; });
    input.addEventListener('change', () => {
      send('core', 'setModuleOption', {
        moduleId: module, option: option,
        value: Number(input.value)
      });
    });
    row.appendChild(label); row.appendChild(input); row.appendChild(out);
    return row;
  }

  function selectField(name, hint, module, option, choices, value) {
    const row = el('div', 'slo-field');
    const label = el('div', 'slo-field__label');
    label.appendChild(el('div', 'slo-field__name', name));
    if (hint) label.appendChild(el('div', 'slo-field__hint', hint));
    const sel = document.createElement('select');
    for (const [val, txt] of choices) {
      const opt = el('option', null, txt);
      opt.value = val;
      sel.appendChild(opt);
    }
    sel.value = value;
    sel.addEventListener('change', () => {
      send('core', 'setModuleOption', {
        moduleId: module, option: option, value: sel.value
      });
    });
    row.appendChild(label); row.appendChild(sel);
    return row;
  }

  function actionField(name, hint, btnLabel, onClick, danger) {
    const row = el('div', 'slo-field');
    const label = el('div', 'slo-field__label');
    label.appendChild(el('div', 'slo-field__name', name));
    if (hint) label.appendChild(el('div', 'slo-field__hint', hint));
    const btn = el('button', 'sl-btn' + (danger ? ' sl-btn--danger' : ''),
                   btnLabel);
    btn.type = 'button';
    btn.addEventListener('click', onClick);



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                         98
      row.appendChild(label); row.appendChild(btn);
      return row;
  }

  function mod(id) { return settings.modules[id] || {}; }

  /* ---- panes ------------------------------------------------------- */
  function paneGeneral() {
    const pane = el('div', 'slo-pane');
    pane.appendChild(el('h2', null, 'General'));
    pane.appendChild(el('p', null,
      'The master switch physically unregisters every module script ' +
      'from every page - pausing SecondLook is a real off, not a mute.'));
    pane.appendChild(toggleField(
      'All modules enabled', 'Unregister everything when off',
      'core',
      () => settings.globalEnabled !== false,
      (v) => send('core', 'setGlobalEnabled', { enabled: v })));
    return pane;
  }

  function paneModule(id, blurb, fields) {
    const pane = el('div', 'slo-pane');
    const info = SL.Registry.byId(id);
    pane.appendChild(el('h2', null, (info && info.name) || id));
    pane.appendChild(el('p', null, blurb));
    pane.appendChild(toggleField(
      'Enabled', null, id,
      () => mod(id).enabled !== false,
      (v) => send('core', 'setModuleEnabled', { moduleId: id, enabled: v })));
    for (const f of fields) pane.appendChild(f);
    return pane;
  }

  function paneSites() {
    const pane = el('div', 'slo-pane');
    pane.appendChild(el('h2', null, 'Site exceptions'));
    pane.appendChild(el('p', null,
      'Per-site disables keep a module dormant on that host: no ' +
      'observers, no DOM footprint. Removing an exception restores ' +
      'default behavior.'));
    const list = el('div');
    const overrides = settings.siteOverrides || {};
    const hosts = Object.keys(overrides);
    if (!hosts.length) {
      list.appendChild(el('div', 'sl-empty', 'No site exceptions yet.'));
    }
    for (const host of hosts) {
      const site = overrides[host].modules || {};
      for (const [moduleId, enabled] of Object.entries(site)) {
        const info = SL.Registry.byId(moduleId);
        const row = el('div', 'slo-field');
        const label = el('div', 'slo-field__label');
        label.appendChild(el('div', 'slo-field__name',
          host + ' - ' + ((info && info.name) || moduleId)));
        label.appendChild(el('div', 'slo-field__hint',
          enabled ? 'forced ON for this site' :
          'dormant on this site (global settings untouched)'));
        const btn = el('button', 'sl-btn', 'Remove');
        btn.type = 'button';
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          send('core', 'clearSiteOverride', { host, moduleId });
          setTimeout(render, 200);
        });
        row.appendChild(label); row.appendChild(btn);
        list.appendChild(row);
      }
    }
    pane.appendChild(list);
    return pane;
  }

  async function paneData() {
    const pane = el('div', 'slo-pane');



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                         99
      pane.appendChild(el('h2', null, 'Your data'));
      pane.appendChild(el('p', null,
        'Everything SecondLook keeps lives in this browser. Event logs ' +
        'store hostnames only - never full URLs, never page content.'));
      const stats = await send('core', 'getStats');
      const s = (stats.ok && stats.data) || { byVerdict: {}, byModule: {} };
      pane.appendChild(el('p', null,
        'Verdicts so far: ' + (s.byVerdict.CLEAR || 0) + ' clear, ' +
        (s.byVerdict.SECOND_LOOK || 0) + ' second looks, ' +
        (s.byVerdict.INTERCEPTED || 0) + ' interceptions.'));
      pane.appendChild(actionField(
        'Reset counters and event log', null, 'Reset', async () => {
          await send('core', 'resetData');
          SLToast.show({ kind: 'ok', title: 'Local data cleared' });
          render();
        }, true));
      pane.appendChild(actionField(
        'Export settings as JSON', null, 'Export', async () => {
          const res = await send('core', 'exportSettings');
          const ta = pane.querySelector('textarea');
          if (ta && res.ok) ta.value = res.data;
        }));
      pane.appendChild(actionField(
        'Import settings from JSON below', null, 'Import', async () => {
          const ta = pane.querySelector('textarea');
          if (!ta || !ta.value.trim()) return;
          await send('core', 'importSettings', { json: ta.value });
          await refresh();
          render();
          SLToast.show({ kind: 'ok', title: 'Settings imported' });
        }));
      const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
      ta.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Settings JSON');
      pane.appendChild(ta);
      return pane;
  }

     ghostClick: () => paneModule('ghostClick',

         mod('tabGuard').threshold || 3),

         'tabGuard', 'windowMs', 5000, 60000, 5000,
         mod('tabGuard').windowMs || 10000)]),
     cookieTamer: () => paneModule('cookieTamer',
       'Never clicks Accept. Clicks only explicit reject controls; ' +
       'otherwise parks the banner behind a reversible bar.',
       [selectField('Strategy', null, 'cookieTamer', 'strategy',
         [['prefer-reject', 'Click honest reject buttons, else park'],
          ['park-only', 'Always park the banner']],
         mod('cookieTamer').strategy || 'prefer-reject')]),
     avIndicator: () => paneModule('avIndicator',
       'A chip whenever a page uses camera, microphone or screen ' +
       'sharing. Only starts and stops cross the world boundary - ' +
       'never page data.', []),
     sites: () => paneSites(),
     data: () => paneData()
  };

  async function render(paneId) {
    await refresh();
    const id = paneId || (location.hash.slice(1) || 'general');
    const builder = PANES[id] || PANES.general;
    main.textContent = '';
    main.appendChild(await builder());
    for (const btn of document.querySelectorAll('.slo-nav__item')) {
      btn.setAttribute('aria-current',
         String(btn.dataset.pane === id));
      btn.onclick = () => {
         location.hash = btn.dataset.pane;
         render(btn.dataset.pane);
      };
    }
  }

  SL.Settings.subscribe(() => render());
  render();
})();

  ui/dashboard.js
/* ui/dashboard.js - read-only control room. Reads storage directly;
 * the few mutations go through the router. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  const send = SL.msg.send;

  function fmtTime(ts) {
    const d = new Date(ts);
    return d.toLocaleDateString() + ' ' + d.toLocaleTimeString();
  }

  function tile(num, label, extraClass) {
    const t = document.createElement('div');
    t.className = 'sl-stat-tile sld-tile ' + (extraClass || '');
    const n = document.createElement('div');
    n.className = 'sl-stat-tile__num';
    n.textContent = String(num);
    const l = document.createElement('div');
    l.className = 'sl-stat-tile__label';
    l.textContent = label;
    t.appendChild(n); t.appendChild(l);
    return t;
  }

  async function renderTiles() {
    const holder = document.getElementById('sld-tiles');
    holder.textContent = '';
    const res = await send('core', 'getStats');
    const s = (res.ok && res.data) || { byVerdict: {}, byModule: {} };
    holder.appendChild(tile(s.byVerdict.CLEAR || 0, 'clear verdicts'));
    holder.appendChild(tile(s.byVerdict.SECOND_LOOK || 0,
      'second looks'));
    holder.appendChild(tile(s.byVerdict.INTERCEPTED || 0,
      'interceptions'));
  }

  function renderModules(stats) {
    const holder = document.getElementById('sld-modules');
    holder.textContent = '';
    for (const mod of SL.Registry.MODULES) {
      if (mod.devOnly) continue;
      const t = document.createElement('div');
      t.className = 'sl-stat-tile sld-tile sld-tile--wide';
      const m = document.createElement('div');
      m.className = 'sld-tile__module';
      m.textContent = mod.name;
      const c = document.createElement('div');
      c.className = 'sld-tile__count';
      c.textContent = String((stats.byModule &&
        stats.byModule[mod.id]) || 0);
      const l = document.createElement('div');
      l.className = 'sl-stat-tile__label';
      l.textContent = 'engine events';
      t.appendChild(m); t.appendChild(c); t.appendChild(l);
      holder.appendChild(t);
    }
  }
  async function renderEvents() {



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                       103
      const tbl = document.getElementById('sld-events');
      tbl.textContent = '';
      const res = await send('core', 'getEvents', { limit: 60 });
      const events = (res.ok && res.data) || [];
      const thead = document.createElement('thead');
      const hrow = document.createElement('tr');
      for (const h of ['When', 'Module', 'Verdict', 'Host', 'Note']) {
        const th = document.createElement('th');
        th.textContent = h;
        hrow.appendChild(th);
      }
      thead.appendChild(hrow);
      tbl.appendChild(thead);
      const tbody = document.createElement('tbody');
      if (!events.length) {
        const row = document.createElement('tr');
        const td = document.createElement('td');
        td.colSpan = 5;
        td.textContent = 'Nothing logged yet - browse a little.';
        row.appendChild(td);
        tbody.appendChild(row);
      }
      for (const e of events) {
        const row = document.createElement('tr');
        for (const v of [fmtTime(e.ts), e.module,
          e.verdict || '-', e.host || '-', e.note || '']) {
          const td = document.createElement('td');
          td.textContent = v;
          row.appendChild(td);
        }
        tbody.appendChild(row);
      }
      tbl.appendChild(tbody);
      return (await send('core', 'getStats')).data;
  }

  document.getElementById('sld-release').onclick = async () => {
     const r = await send('tabGuard', 'releaseGroup');
     SLToast.show({
       kind: 'ok', title: 'Held tabs released',
       body: r.ok ? 'The storm pen is empty.' : 'Nothing was held.'
     });
  };
  document.getElementById('sld-reset').onclick = async () => {
     await send('core', 'resetData');
     await renderAll();
  };
  document.getElementById('sld-options').onclick = () => {
     chrome.runtime.openOptionsPage();
  };

  async function renderAll() {
    await renderTiles();
    const stats = await renderEvents();
    renderModules(stats || {});
  }

  renderAll();
  setInterval(renderAll, 15000);          // gentle live refresh
})();



11.4 Popup polish + two new router routes
The popup's final form enables the Dashboard button and adds two per-site actions to the site card. Show ghosts asks the
service worker to message the tab, which flashes every neutralized overlay for 2.5 seconds (Part 5's listener does the flashing).
Sweep cookies invokes Part 9's sweep for the current tab and toasts the kept/removed counts. Both are one route away - paste
the block into the router first, then replace ui/popup.js wholesale with the version below.




SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                      104
  background/module-router.js - add after avIndicator routes
  register('core', {
    /* Popup: flash neutralized ghost overlays in one tab. */
    async revealGhosts(payload) {
       await chrome.tabs.sendMessage(payload.tabId, {
         module: 'ghostClick', type: 'flashOverlays'
       });
       return { sent: true };
    },
    /* Popup: user-invoked cookie sweep for the current site. */
    async sweepCookies(payload) {
       return (await HANDLERS.cookieTamer.sweepCurrentSite[0](payload));
    }
  });

  ui/popup.js - final version (replace Part 0's file)
/* ui/popup.js - final popup: pillars, live rows, site actions. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  const send = SL.msg.send;
  const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);

  const state = { tabId: null, snapshot: null, settings: null };

  function eyeIcon() {
    return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="16" height="16" ' +
       'aria-hidden="true"><path d="M2 12s3.5-6 10-6 10 6 10 6-3.5 ' +
       '6-10 6S2 12 2 12z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" ' +
       'stroke-width="1.8"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="2.6" ' +
       'fill="currentColor"/></svg>';
  }
  function iconFor(id) {
    const paths = {
       demoGreeting: '', linkSniper: '',
       redirectDetective: 'M9 15l6-6M8 12l-2 2a3 3 0 004 4l2-2' +
         'M16 12l2-2a3 3 0 00-4-4l-2 2',
       trustBadge: 'M12 3l2.5 2 3 .5.5 3L20 12l-2 2.5-.5 3-3 .5' +
         '-2.5 2-2.5-2-3-.5-.5-3L4 12l2-2.5.5-3 3-.5zM9 12l2 2 4-4',
       formGuardian: 'M4 6h16v12H4zM7 10h10M7 14h6',
       ghostClick: 'M4 4h16v16H4z',
       fakeDownload: 'M12 4v10m0 0l-4-4m4 4l4-4M5 19h14',
       downloadGuard: 'M12 3l7 3v6c0 4-3 7-7 9-4-2-7-5-7-9V6z' +
         'M12 9v4',
       tabGuard: 'M3 7h18v13H3zM3 7l3-4h8l3 4',
       cookieTamer: 'M12 4a8 8 0 100 16 8 8 0 000-16z' +
         'M10 10h.01M14 13h.01M9 15h.01',
       avIndicator: 'M3 7h12v10H3zM15 11l6-3v8l-6-3z'
    };
    return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="16" height="16" ' +
       'fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" ' +
       'aria-hidden="true">' +
       (paths[id] === '' ? eyeIcon().replace(/<\/?svg[^>]*>/g, '')
        : '<path d="' + (paths[id] || '') + '"/>') +
       (paths[id] === '' ? '' : '') + '</svg>';
  }

  async function init() {
    const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true,
                                            currentWindow: true });
    state.tabId = tab ? tab.id : null;
    const res = await send('core', 'getSnapshot', { tabId: state.tabId });
    state.snapshot = res.ok ? res.data : null;
    state.settings = state.snapshot && state.snapshot.settings;
    renderSite();
    renderPillars();
    renderGlobal();
    chrome.storage.onChanged.addListener(onStorage);
  }

  function onStorage(changes, area) {
    if (area === 'local' && changes['sl.settings']) {
      state.settings = changes['sl.settings'].newValue;
      renderPillars();


SecondLook - Build Guide                                                     105
        renderGlobal();
      }
      if (area === 'session' && changes['sl.status']) {
        if (state.snapshot) {
          state.snapshot.statusLines =
            (changes['sl.status'].newValue || {})[state.tabId] || {};
        }
        renderPillars();
      }
  }

  function renderGlobal() {
    const on = !!(state.settings && state.settings.globalEnabled !== false);
    $('slp-global').checked = on;
    $('slp-paused').hidden = on;
  }

  function renderSite() {
    const snap = state.snapshot;
    const isHttp = snap && /^https?:/i.test(snap.url || '');
    $('slp-site').hidden = !isHttp;
    $('slp-empty').hidden = !!isHttp;
    if (!isHttp) return;
    $('slp-host').textContent = snap.host || '(this page)';
    const holder = $('slp-verdict');
    holder.textContent = '';
    if (snap.pageVerdict) {
      const v = snap.pageVerdict;
      const pill = SLVerdict.pill(v.verdict,
        v.verdict === 'CLEAR' ? 'OK' : 'Take a look');
      if (v.reasons && v.reasons.length) {
        pill.title = v.reasons.join(' ');
      }
      holder.appendChild(pill);
    }
    updateSafeModeButton();
  }

  function updateSafeModeButton() {
    const btn = $('slp-safemode');
    const on = !!(state.snapshot && state.snapshot.safeModeOn);
    btn.textContent = on ? 'Safe Mode is ON - unfreeze' : 'Safe Mode this tab';
    btn.classList.toggle('sl-btn--primary', on);
  }

  function renderPillars() {
    const nav = $('slp-pillars');
    nav.textContent = '';
    SL.Registry.PILLARS.forEach((pillar, idx) => {
      const section = document.createElement('section');
      section.className = 'sl-pillar';
      section.dataset.collapsed = idx > 0 ? 'true' : 'false';
      const head = document.createElement('button');
      head.className = 'slp-pillar__head';
      head.type = 'button';
      head.setAttribute('aria-expanded', idx > 0 ? 'false' : 'true');
      head.innerHTML = '<span>' + pillar.name + '</span>' +
        '<svg class="slp-pillar__chev" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="12" ' +
        'height="12" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 9l6 6 6-6" ' +
        'fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"/></svg>';
      head.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const now = section.dataset.collapsed !== 'true';
        section.dataset.collapsed = String(now);
        head.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(!now));
      });
      section.appendChild(head);
      const rows = document.createElement('div');
      rows.className = 'slp-pillar__rows';
      for (const mod of SL.Registry.MODULES) {
        if (mod.pillar !== pillar.id) continue;
        rows.appendChild(makeRow(mod));
      }
      section.appendChild(rows);
      nav.appendChild(section);



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                          106
      });
  }

  function makeRow(mod) {
    const row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'sl-row';
    row.dataset.module = mod.id;
    row.title = mod.blurb;

      const icon = document.createElement('div');
      icon.className = 'sl-row__icon';
      icon.innerHTML = iconFor(mod.id);
      row.appendChild(icon);

      const body = document.createElement('div');
      body.className = 'sl-row__body';
      const name = document.createElement('div');
      name.className = 'sl-row__name';
      name.textContent = mod.name;
      if (mod.devOnly) {
        const tag = document.createElement('span');
        tag.className = 'sl-row__dev-tag';
        tag.textContent = 'DEV';
        tag.style.marginLeft = '6px';
        name.appendChild(tag);
      }
      body.appendChild(name);
      const status = document.createElement('div');
      status.className = 'sl-row__status';
      status.textContent = statusText(mod.id);
      body.appendChild(status);
      row.appendChild(body);

      const toggle = document.createElement('label');
      toggle.className = 'sl-toggle';
      const input = document.createElement('input');
      input.type = 'checkbox';
      input.setAttribute('aria-label', mod.name + ' on this browser');
      input.checked = isOn(mod.id);
      input.addEventListener('change', () =>
        onToggle(mod.id, input.checked, row));
      const track = document.createElement('span');
      track.className = 'sl-toggle__track';
      toggle.appendChild(input);
      toggle.appendChild(track);
      row.appendChild(toggle);
      return row;
  }

  function isOn(moduleId) {
    return !!(state.settings &&
      SL.Settings.isModuleOn(state.settings, moduleId, null));
  }

  function statusText(moduleId) {
    const lines = state.snapshot && state.snapshot.statusLines;
    const line = lines && lines[moduleId];
    if (line) return line;
    return isOn(moduleId) ? 'Idle' : 'Off';
  }

  async function onToggle(moduleId, enabled, rowEl) {
    rowEl.classList.add('sl-row--syncing');
    const statusEl = rowEl.querySelector('.sl-row__status');
    if (statusEl) statusEl.textContent = 'syncing';
    await send('core', 'setModuleEnabled', { moduleId, enabled });
  }

  $('slp-global').addEventListener('change', async (e) => {
    await send('core', 'setGlobalEnabled', { enabled: e.target.checked });
  });

  $('slp-safemode').addEventListener('click', async () => {
    const on = !(state.snapshot && state.snapshot.safeModeOn);



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                     107
    await send('core', 'safeMode', { tabId: state.tabId, on });
    state.snapshot.safeModeOn = on;
    renderSite();
  });

  $('slp-ghosts').addEventListener('click', async () => {
    const res = await send('core', 'revealGhosts', { tabId: state.tabId });
    SLToast.show({
      kind: res.ok ? 'ok' : 'warn',
      title: res.ok ? 'Neutralized overlays flashed in the page'
                    : 'No page to flash',
      body: res.ok ? 'Look for the amber outlines.' :
        'Open a normal http(s) page first.',
      timeoutMs: 4000
    });
  });

  $('slp-sweep').addEventListener('click', async () => {
    const res = await send('core', 'sweepCookies', { tabId: state.tabId });
    if (res.ok) {
      SLToast.show({
        kind: 'ok', title: 'Cookie sweep complete',
        body: 'Kept ' + res.data.kept + ' (login/session), removed ' +
              res.data.removed + '.'
      });
    } else {
      SLToast.show({ kind: 'warn', title: 'Sweep unavailable',
        body: 'Open a normal http(s) site first.' });
    }
  });

  $('slp-options').addEventListener('click', () => {
    chrome.runtime.openOptionsPage();
  });
  $('slp-dashboard').addEventListener('click', () => {
    chrome.tabs.create({ url: chrome.runtime.getURL('/ui/dashboard.html') });
  });

  init();
})();

 • Reload the extension. Right-click the toolbar icon and open Options: every pane should render current values; flip Link
   Sniper's hover delay and confirm the popup status cadence changes on your next page hover.




SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                     108
 • Add a site exception from the console of any site: run SL.msg.send('core','setSiteOverride',{host:location.h
   ostname,moduleId:'ghostClick',enabled:false}) in the page console - then check the Site exceptions pane lists it,
   remove it, and confirm the module wakes up again.
 • Open the Dashboard from the popup. Stat tiles show the counts accumulated during Parts 1-10 testing; the events table
   lists hostnames with notes; Release held tabs empties any leftover storm pen.
 • On a page where Part 5 neutralized ghosts, click Show ghosts in the popup: amber outlines flash in the page for 2.5
   seconds.
 • On any logged-in site, click Sweep cookies: the toast reports kept vs removed counts, and reloading the page keeps you
   logged in (that is the whole point of the preservation rules).

Part 12 - Test Pages & Local Test Server

12.1 Design analysis
Every module in this guide was verified against pages that deliberately exhibit the hostile pattern - and now you get the same
bench. The kit is one small Python server plus seven static pages. The server exists because two behaviors cannot be faked
with static files: real HTTP 302 redirect chains (Redirect Detective needs genuine Location hops) and files whose bytes
contradict their names (Download Guard needs a MZ-byte file named .pdf). serve-tests.py provides both, plus a POST
sink for Form Guardian, and binds on 0.0.0.0:8000 so localhost:8000 and 127.0.0.1:8000 are *different origins* -
which is exactly what the fake-login page needs to demo cross-origin credential submission without a second server.

Run it from the extension root with python3 test-pages/serve-tests.py, then browse http://localhost:8000/ for
the index. Content scripts match http://*/*, so localhost is fully covered by every module with zero configuration.


12.2 The test server
  test-pages/serve-tests.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""test-pages/serve-tests.py - local test bench for SecondLook.

Serves ./test-pages on 0.0.0.0:8000 with:
  /hop/1 -> 302 -> /hop/2 -> 302 -> /hop/3   (real redirect chain)
  /sink                                      (POST echo, cross-origin target)
  /files/dummy.pdf                           (real PDF magic bytes)
  /files/evil-invoice.pdf                    (MZ bytes, PDF name)
  /files/plain.txt                           (honest file)
"""
import os
import socketserver
from http.server import SimpleHTTPRequestHandler

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
PDF = b"%PDF-1.4\n1 0 obj<</Type/Catalog/Pages 2 0 R>>endobj\n" \
      b"2 0 obj<</Type/Pages/Kids[3 0 R]>>endobj\n" \
      b"3 0 obj<</Type/Page/Parent 2 0 R>>endobj\ntrailer<</Root 1 0 R>>\n"
MZ = b"MZ\x90\x00\x03\x00\x00\x00\x04\x00\x00\x00\xff\xff\x00\x00" \
     b"This is a Windows program wearing a PDF costume. " * 8
class Handler(SimpleHTTPRequestHandler):



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                    109
     def __init__(self, *a, **kw):
         super().__init__(*a, directory=HERE, **kw)

     def do_GET(self):
         if self.path == "/hop/1":
             self.send_response(302)
             self.send_header("Location", "/hop/2")
             self.end_headers()
         elif self.path == "/hop/2":
             self.send_response(302)
             self.send_header("Location", "/hop/3")
             self.end_headers()
         elif self.path == "/hop/3":
             body = b"final destination - no more redirects"
             self.send_response(200)
             self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/plain")
             self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
             self.end_headers()
             self.wfile.write(body)
         elif self.path == "/files/dummy.pdf":
             self._bytes(PDF, "application/pdf")
         elif self.path == "/files/evil-invoice.pdf":
             self._bytes(MZ, "application/pdf")
         elif self.path == "/files/plain.txt":
             self._bytes(b"an honest little text file\n", "text/plain")
         else:
             super().do_GET()

     def do_POST(self):
         if self.path == "/sink":
             length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length") or 0)
             data = self.rfile.read(length) if length else b""
             body = b"credentials received: " + data
             self.send_response(200)
             self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/plain")
             self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
             self.end_headers()
             self.wfile.write(body)
         else:
             self.send_response(404)
             self.end_headers()

     def _bytes(self, payload, ctype):
         self.send_response(200)
         self.send_header("Content-Type", ctype)
         self.send_header("Content-Disposition", 'attachment')
         self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(payload)))
         self.end_headers()
         self.wfile.write(payload)

     def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
         print("[serve-tests]", fmt % args)


class Server(socketserver.ThreadingTCPServer):
    allow_reuse_address = True


if __name__ == "__main__":
    addr = ("0.0.0.0", 8000)
    print("SecondLook test bench on http://localhost:8000/ " +
          "(also http://127.0.0.1:8000/ - a DIFFERENT origin)")
    with Server(addr, Handler) as httpd:
        httpd.serve_forever()

SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                    115
Part 13 - Ship It: README & Store Listing

13.1 Design analysis
The last mile is where security extensions die: a reviewer reads the permission list with no context, a user reads the store
listing with no patience, and both decide in under a minute. This part is the complete text for both audiences, plus the final
housekeeping: removing the dev-only demo module, packaging the zip, and the screenshot checklist. Nothing here is code you
paste into the extension runtime except the README itself - everything else is paste-ready copy for the Web Store forms.


13.2 README.md
  README.md
# SecondLook

Quality-of-life tools that quietly watch your back.

We don't tell you that the internet is dangerous. We tell you when
something deserves a second look.

## What it does

SecondLook is ten small tools in one extension:

- **Link & Site Intel** - Link Sniper (hover verdicts on links),
  Redirect Detective (traces redirect chains hop by hop), Trust Badge
  (flags trust seals that are just images).
- **Form & Click Safety** - Form Guardian (stops passwords being sent
  to a different domain than the page you are on), Ghost Click
  Blocker (neutralizes invisible click-stealing overlays), Fake
  Download Button (marks competing download decoys).
- **Files & Downloads** - Download Guard (checks the first bytes of
  downloaded files against their names).
- **Annoyance & Privacy** - Tab Guard (contains popup storms in a
  collapsible group), Cookie Tamer (parks consent banners - it never
  clicks Accept), Webcam/Mic Indicator (a chip whenever camera,
  microphone or screen sharing is in use).

## Privacy - the short version

- All analysis happens on this device. Nothing is uploaded, ever.
- Zero telemetry, zero analytics, zero remote code. No eval, no
  bundled scripts loaded from the network.
- Local storage holds: your settings, a verdict counter, a capped
  event log of **hostnames only** (never full URLs), and salted
  hashes of form destinations you chose to trust. Clear it any time
  from Settings -> Your data.
- Turning a module off is real: the extension unregisters that
  module's code from every page, not merely hides its warnings.
  Per-site exceptions leave the module dormant (no observers, no
  DOM footprint) on that site.
- Blocking is used in exactly one place: Safe Mode, a per-tab
  network freeze you trigger yourself, removed with one click.

## Honest limitations

- Form Guardian inspects native form submissions; JavaScript
  exfiltration via fetch/XHR is out of scope.
- Download Guard does not look inside archives.
- The camera/mic indicator detects use that starts after a page
  loads with the module enabled; it cannot see streams that began
  before, and unhooking already-loaded pages requires a reload.
- Cookie Tamer clicks only buttons that explicitly reject; when none
  exists it parks the banner and lets you decide.

## Install (development)
1. Chrome 116 or newer. `chrome://extensions` -> Developer mode.



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                   116
2. Generate icons: `python3 tools/make-icons.py` (needs Pillow).
3. Load unpacked -> select this folder.
4. Optional: `python3 test-pages/serve-tests.py` and open
   http://localhost:8000 for the full test bench.

## Layout

     manifest.json             MV3 manifest; content_scripts is [] by design
     background/               service worker + router + bootstrap + guards
     shared/                   storage, settings, messaging, registry, bridge
     engine/                   verdict engine (the only URL scorer)
     ui/                       design tokens, components, popup, options,
                              dashboard
     modules/                  one folder per module
     test-pages/               local test bench (not shipped)

## Troubleshooting

- A module does nothing on a site: check the popup row - it may be
  off globally or dormant for that site.
- Everything silent everywhere: the master switch in the popup.
- Weird behavior after editing code: reload the extension; the
  service worker console (chrome://extensions -> SecondLook ->
  service worker) tells the truth.



13.3 Remove the demo module
The demo chip is a development proof, not a product feature. Removal is three small edits and one deletion - and after each,
reload the extension and confirm the popup still renders ten rows plus no DEV tag.

 • Delete the folder modules/demo-greeting/.
 • In shared/module-registry.js, delete the entire demoGreeting entry from MODULES (the first object in the array,
   marked with devOnly: true).
 • In ui/popup.js, delete the two demo references: the demoGreeting: eyeIcon(), line inside ICONS and, if you copied
   the Part 11 version, nothing else - iconFor falls back safely.
 • In shared/settings.js, delete the demoGreeting: { enabled: true }, line from DEFAULTS. Existing installs keep a
   stale key in storage harmlessly; the registry no longer iterates it.


13.4 Web Store listing copy
  (store listing - paste into the developer dashboard forms)
Name:
  SecondLook - a second look before you click

Short summary (132 chars max):
  Hover verdicts on links, storm-proof tabs, honest cookie banners -
  quality-of-life tools that quietly watch your back. Local-only.

Category: Productivity

Detailed description:
  SecondLook is a suite of ten quiet tools that watch the web pages
  you already use, and speak up only when something deserves a
  second look.

  LINKS AND SITES
  - Hover any link for a plain-language verdict: "Nothing unusual",
    or one sentence on what looks off (lookalike domains, disguised
    text, raw IP destinations).
  - Right-click any link to trace its full redirect chain, hop by
    hop.
  - Trust seals that are just images get marked - because anyone
    can paste a picture of a padlock.

  FORMS AND CLICKS



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                 117
  - If a login form would send your password to a different domain
    than the page you are looking at, a full-page card stops it:
    Continue or Stop. Always reversible. Always your call.
  - Invisible overlays that steal your clicks are quietly defused.
  - Competing "Download" decoy buttons are marked so the real one
    stands out.

  FILES
  - A file named invoice.pdf whose bytes are actually a program
    never reaches your disk. Every stop has an Undo.

  ANNOYANCE AND PRIVACY
  - Popup storms get contained in a collapsed "Held for review" tab
    group instead of your face.
  - Consent banners: we click only buttons that explicitly reject.
    If there is no honest reject button, the banner is parked -
    never accepted on your behalf.
  - A clear indicator whenever a page uses your camera, microphone
    or screen sharing.

  PRIVACY
  - Everything runs on your device. No telemetry, no analytics, no
    remote code, no account.
  - Every module can be turned off - and off means the code is
    really removed from pages, not just silenced.
  - One blocking feature exists at all: Safe Mode, a per-tab network
    freeze you trigger yourself.

  We don't tell you that the internet is dangerous. We tell you
  when something deserves a second look.



13.5 Permission justification (paste into the review form)
  (review form - permission justifications)
storage: settings, local counters, and a capped hostname-only event
  log on the user's device. Nothing syncs, nothing uploads.
tabs: show the current site's state in our popup and contain
  popup-storm tab spam in a review group.
scripting: register/unregister each module's code on pages; this is
  how turning a module off truly removes it. No remote code - only
  files bundled in this package.
downloads: verify the first 16 bytes of a download against its file
  name, and stop (reversibly) documents whose bytes are programs.
cookies: the user-invoked "sweep this site's cookies" action, which
  preserves login and session cookies.
alarms: prune the local event log periodically. No network use.
contextMenus: "Where does this link really go?" and the per-tab Safe
  Mode toggle.
tabGroups: the collapsed "Held for review" group for popup storms.
declarativeNetRequest: Safe Mode only - a session rule scoped to one
  tab that the user enables and removes with a click. Our only
  blocking API, never used automatically.
webRequest: observe redirect hops to draw the chain a link really
  takes. Observer only; no blocking, no header modification.
http/https on all sites: the tool is site-agnostic by design - link
  and form checks must work everywhere the user browses. All
  analysis is local; no data leaves the browser.



13.6 Screenshot checklist
Store listings convert on their screenshots, and you already have every scene built. Capture at 1280x800, light mode, in this
order: (1) the popup open on link-farm.html with an amber pill and a green pill visible; (2) the redirect trace card
mid-hop-list; (3) the Form Guardian red card on fake-login.html; (4) fake-download.html with decoys outlined; (5) the
toggle-test page mid-cleanup - modules flipping off with effects vanishing; (6) the dashboard with populated stats. The fifth
one is the money shot: record it as a screen capture if the dashboard supports video listings, because one-click cleanup is the
single most persuasive thing this extension does.



SecondLook - Build Guide                                                                                                    118
13.7 Packaging
The shipped zip contains the runtime tree and nothing else. Run from the parent folder of secondlook/:

  (terminal - package for the store)
cd secondlook
python3 tools/make-icons.py          # ensure icons exist and are fresh
cd ..
zip -r secondlook-1.0.0.zip secondlook \
  -x "secondlook/test-pages/*" \
  -x "secondlook/tools/*" \
  -x "secondlook/README.md"          # store listing carries this now

Before uploading, walk one final pass with test-pages/serve-tests.py against the packaged build loaded from a fresh
unpacked copy of the zip's contents, then submit. Review turnaround for permission-rich extensions is typically a few days;
the justification block above answers every question they will ask, because it is the same honesty the code was built with.
